"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Loader2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useUserPermissions, type PermissionFlag } from "@/hooks/useUserPermissions";
import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useOrbitalActive } from "@/contexts/OrbitalActiveContext";
import PracticeInsightStage from "@/components/mainhub/PracticeInsightStage";
import AnalyticsStage from "@/components/mainhub/AnalyticsStage";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { useQuestionBank } from "@/contexts/QuestionBankContext";
import TodayFocusPanel from "@/components/mainhub/TodayFocusPanel";
import { QuestionPicker } from "@/components/drill/QuestionPicker";
import QuestionJump from "@/components/questionfinder/QuestionJump";
import QuestionFinder from "@/components/questionfinder/QuestionFinder";
import { SectionToggle } from "@/components/drill/SectionToggle";
import { RcHubSurface } from "@/features/rc/components/RcHubSurface";
import {
  DRILL_SECTION_PARAM,
  rememberSection,
  resolveDrillSection,
  type DrillSection,
} from "@/lib/drillSection";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { TypeDrillConfig } from "@/types/drill";

/**
 * The greeting name, resolved once per mount: profiles.display_name, falling
 * back to metadata then email. Lifted out of the hero because the hero
 * remounts on every section flip, and re-querying profiles to redraw the same
 * name would be a database round trip per toggle.
 */
function useGreetingName() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [profileDisplayName, setProfileDisplayName] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("profiles")
        .select("display_name")
        .eq("class_id", user.id)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (!cancelled) setProfileDisplayName(data?.display_name ?? null);
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [user]);

  return React.useMemo(() => {
    const raw =
      profileDisplayName ||
      (user?.user_metadata?.display_name as string | undefined) ||
      user?.email?.split("@")[0] ||
      "";
    const name = raw.split(/[\s._-]/)[0].replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, "");
    return name ? name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + name.slice(1).toLowerCase() : "";
  }, [profileDisplayName, user]);
}

function MainhubHero({
  section,
  firstName,
  drillReturnTo,
  onOpenBuilder,
  onStartSmartRc,
}: {
  section: DrillSection;
  firstName: string;
  drillReturnTo: string;
  onOpenBuilder: () => void;
  onStartSmartRc: () => void;
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const todayLabel = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
        weekday: "long",
        month: "long",
        day: "numeric",
      }),
    [],
  );

  const startSmartDrill = React.useCallback(() => {
    navigate("/drill", { state: { mode: "adaptive", returnTo: drillReturnTo } });
  }, [drillReturnTo, navigate]);

  return (
    /* Surface, scrim and grid live in .drill-observatory-hero / -hero-grid: the
       horizon image needs image-set() and a two-layer scrim pseudo-element,
       neither of which belongs in a className. */
    <Card
      data-section={section}
      className="drill-observatory-hero w-full border-0 bg-transparent p-0 shadow-none"
    >
      <div className="drill-observatory-hero-grid">
        <div className="drill-observatory-greeting flex flex-col justify-end">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-[9px]">
            <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-50">
              {todayLabel}
            </p>
            {/* Size, face and the italic on the name come from
                .drill-observatory-greeting h2 — the CSS rule outranks utilities
                here, so putting them in className would be dead code. */}
            <h2>
              {firstName ? (
                <>
                  Welcome back, <em>{firstName}</em>
                </>
              ) : (
                "Welcome back"
              )}
            </h2>
            {/* Says what the button below actually does. It used to open
                "Pick up where you left off" on both sections, and neither CTA
                resumes anything — resuming lives in Today, one card over. */}
            <p className="drill-observatory-ink-62 text-sm leading-[1.55]">
              {section === "rc"
                ? "Read one passage now, untimed, with the answers after it. Or build a set of passages around exactly what you want to read."
                : "Start an adaptive set that follows your weakest question types, or build a drill around exactly what you want to practice."}
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button
            onClick={section === "rc" ? onStartSmartRc : startSmartDrill}
            className="drill-observatory-cta mt-5 w-fit"
          >
            {/* RC's "smart" is one untimed passage, not the adaptive engine
                LR's button opens. Same word, two promises — so it says what it
                starts. */}
            {section === "rc" ? "Start One Passage" : "Start Smart Drill"}
            <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden />
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="drill-observatory-console w-full">
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col justify-between gap-6">
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <h3 className="text-[clamp(1.35rem,3cqi,2rem)] font-semibold leading-tight tracking-[-0.025em] text-[#f4f1ea]">
                {section === "rc" ? "Build the reading set you need." : "Build the drill you need."}
              </h3>
              <p className="drill-observatory-ink-62 max-w-[48ch] text-sm leading-[1.6]">
                {section === "rc"
                  ? "Choose PrepTests, topics, formats, frameworks, and difficulty independently. Hand-pick the exact passages, or let the set fill itself."
                  : "Choose PrepTests, question types, reasoning, features, and levels independently. Preview the exact set before you start."}
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button
              type="button"
              onClick={onOpenBuilder}
              className="drill-observatory-console-start"
            >
              {section === "rc" ? "Set up an RC drill" : "Set up an LR drill"}
              <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

/**
 * Launcher stage — hosts the Build a Set panel. Sitting a real section is NOT
 * here: that is the Testing Centre's job, and having both doors made the drill
 * hub the second place to do the same thing.
 */
function LauncherStage({
  kind,
  section,
  drillReturnTo,
  onCancel,
}: {
  kind: "type-drill";
  section: DrillSection;
  drillReturnTo: string;
  onCancel: () => void;
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { manifest, isLoading, error } = useQuestionBank();

  const start = React.useCallback(
    (mode: "type-drill", config: TypeDrillConfig) => {
      navigate("/drill", { state: { mode, config, returnTo: drillReturnTo } });
    },
    [drillReturnTo, navigate],
  );

  /* RC's builder reads the passage index, not the LR question bank, so it
     returns before `useQuestionBank`'s loading gate — waiting on 5.5 MB of LR
     questions to draw a reading drill would be a spinner for nothing. */
  if (section === "rc") {
    return (
      <div className="drill-observatory-launcher relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="relative z-10">
          <RcHubSurface embedded showHistory={false} onCancel={onCancel} />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="drill-observatory-launcher relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* No header here on purpose: QuestionPicker renders its own title and
          Cancel control, so adding one would show two titles and two Cancels. */}
      <div className="relative z-10">
        {isLoading && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-14">
            <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-white/50" aria-hidden />
            <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-45">
              Loading questions…
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {!isLoading && error && (
          <div className="py-14 text-center text-sm font-medium text-white/60">{error}</div>
        )}

        {!isLoading && !error && manifest && (
          <div className="drill-observatory-launcher-panel">
            <QuestionPicker
              manifest={manifest}
              onStartDrill={(config) => start("type-drill", config)}
              onCancel={onCancel}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Drill hub stage. Renders inside the persistent Classroom frame.
 */
export default function AcademyMainhub({
  drillReturnTo = "/classroom",
}: {
  drillReturnTo?: string;
}) {
  const perms = useUserPermissions();
  const { setActive: setOrbitalActive } = useOrbitalActive();
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const firstName = useGreetingName();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  /* Which section the hub is showing. URL first, then what this browser
     remembers, then LR — see `src/lib/drillSection.ts`. Reading it from the
     URL rather than from state is what makes a refresh, a Back press, and a
     shared link all land in the same place. */
  const sectionParam = searchParams.get(DRILL_SECTION_PARAM);
  const section = React.useMemo(() => resolveDrillSection(sectionParam), [sectionParam]);

  /* A bare URL states no section, so it resolves from what this browser
     remembers — and memory MOVES. Land on /classroom/drill in LR, throw the
     switch to RC, then press Back: the bare entry re-resolves against the
     now-RC memory and Back appears to do nothing.

     So the first thing the hub does is write the resolved section into the
     URL, replacing the entry rather than pushing one. Every history entry then
     states its own section and Back is deterministic. Runs once: the guard is
     false as soon as the param exists. */
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (sectionParam != null && sectionParam.trim() !== "") return;
    const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    next.set(DRILL_SECTION_PARAM, section);
    setSearchParams(next, { replace: true });
  }, [section, sectionParam, searchParams, setSearchParams]);

  const stageParam = searchParams.get("stage");
  const stage =
    stageParam === "analytics"
      ? "analytics"
      : stageParam === "type-drill"
      ? stageParam
      : "default";
  const isLauncherStage = stage === "type-drill";

  /* Sitting a section moved to the Testing Centre. Old links, bookmarks and
     anything a student had open land there rather than silently falling back
     to the hub's default stage with no explanation. */
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (stageParam === "full-section") navigate("/testing", { replace: true });
  }, [stageParam, navigate]);
  /* Build a Set needs the whole canvas: its picker is a dense two-column panel
     that got its Set Summary column clipped inside the ~600px bay. */
  const isWideLauncherStage = stage === "type-drill";
  const analyticsTab = searchParams.get("tab") ?? "overview";

  const can = React.useCallback(
    (flag: PermissionFlag) => perms.loading || perms.is_admin || !!perms[flag],
    [perms],
  );
  const canOpenAnalytics = can("has_analytics_access");
  const canOpenJournal = can("has_journal_access");

  /* Throwing the switch REPLACES the query rather than merging into it: an LR
     launcher stage has no RC counterpart at the same key, so carrying `stage`
     across would land a student on a panel that does not exist for the section
     they just chose. Pushed, not replaced, so Back returns to the section they
     came from.

     The switch is unconditional. A half-built LR set is discarded without a
     prompt — Joshua's call, 2026-08-26: a flip that stops to ask is not a
     flip. */
  const changeSection = React.useCallback(
    (next: DrillSection) => {
      rememberSection(next);
      setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: next }, { replace: false });
    },
    [setSearchParams],
  );

  const openAnalytics = React.useCallback(
    (tab: string = "overview") => {
      setSearchParams(
        { [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: "analytics", tab },
        { replace: false },
      );
    },
    [section, setSearchParams],
  );

  const setAnalyticsTab = React.useCallback(
    (tab: string) => {
      setSearchParams(
        { [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: "analytics", tab },
        { replace: true },
      );
    },
    [section, setSearchParams],
  );

  const openLauncher = React.useCallback(
    (kind: "type-drill") => {
      setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section, stage: kind }, { replace: false });
    },
    [section, setSearchParams],
  );

  const closeStage = React.useCallback(() => {
    setSearchParams({ [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: section }, { replace: true });
  }, [section, setSearchParams]);

  /* Smart Reading Drill: one passage, untimed, answers after each passage —
     RcHubSurface's own defaults, spelled in the URL so the builder mounts,
     applies them, and starts itself. The student sees the builder for the
     moment the passage index is loading and then lands in the drill; they
     never have to press Start a second time. */
  const startSmartRc = React.useCallback(() => {
    rememberSection("rc");
    setSearchParams(
      {
        [DRILL_SECTION_PARAM]: "rc",
        stage: "type-drill",
        count: "1",
        timing: "untimed",
        reveal: "per_passage",
        start: "1",
      },
      { replace: false },
    );
  }, [setSearchParams]);

  // The analytics stage takes over the below-hero area; suppress mainhub
  // overlays (chat widget) the same way an expanded orbital node used to.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    setOrbitalActive(stage !== "default");
    return () => setOrbitalActive(false);
  }, [setOrbitalActive, stage]);

  return (
    <div className="drill-observatory-root @container relative h-full w-full overflow-y-auto bg-transparent px-0 py-0 pb-12">
      {/* 1140px and a 20px gap: the design's column. The three cards each carry
          26-30px of internal padding, so the gap between them stays smaller than
          their padding by design — they read as three objects on one field, not
          as one split surface. */}
      <div className="drill-observatory-canvas relative mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[71.25rem] flex-col gap-5">
        {/* The address row rides above the hero rather than inside it: the
            route label row is decorative and aria-hidden, and the hero's two
            columns are already the page's two primary actions. It sits in the
            same place it occupies on Classroom Home — the band directly under
            the top of the surface — so it is one control in one position across
            both mounts. No access check here: the whole route is behind
            has_drill_access. */}
        <div className="drill-observatory-tools">
          <QuestionJump
            returnTo={drillReturnTo}
            tone="dark"
            bank={section}
            withSearch={false}
          />
          {/* Search and the section switch are one cluster hard right, on the
              same line. The search trigger used to ride at the end of the
              address row, which parked it directly above the LR/RC switch and
              read as a stack of two unrelated controls. Side by side they read
              as what they are: the two utilities of this band. */}
          <div className="drill-observatory-tools-aside">
            <QuestionFinder returnTo={drillReturnTo} tone="dark" bank={section} />
            <SectionToggle section={section} onChange={changeSection} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* The same hero stays mounted across section changes. Swapping its
            content in place prevents the LR/RC control from producing a flash
            or an intermediate empty frame. */}
        <MainhubHero
          section={section}
          firstName={firstName}
          drillReturnTo={drillReturnTo}
          onOpenBuilder={() => openLauncher("type-drill")}
          onStartSmartRc={startSmartRc}
        />

        {stage === "analytics" ? (
          <section
            className="drill-observatory-analytics"
            aria-label="Analytics workspace"
            data-tour="mainhub-analytics"
          >
            <AnalyticsStage
              tab={analyticsTab}
              section={section}
              onTabChange={setAnalyticsTab}
            />
          </section>
        ) : (
          /* A launcher swaps out ONLY the practice bay. Today (assignments,
             notes, notification items) is live status and stays visible while
             the student sets a drill up — replacing the whole deck made it look
             like the card had been deleted.

             Build a Set is the exception: its picker is a dense two-column
             panel that got its Set Summary column clipped outright inside the
             ~600px bay. For that stage the deck stacks — the picker takes the
             full canvas width and Today drops beneath it, still in sight.

             `items-stretch` (the default), not `items-start`: the two cards are
             halves of one deck row and must share its height, or the shorter one
             stops short and the row's bottom edge reads as ragged.

             1.2:1, not the old 1.72:1. The wide column was holding the sparse
             content (two tools, two links, one insight row) while Today —
             headline, three assignment rows, status badges — was squeezed into
             382px and wrapping. The split was feeding space to the column that
             needed it least. */
          <section
            className={cn(
              "drill-observatory-deck grid grid-cols-1 items-stretch",
              !isWideLauncherStage &&
                "@[760px]:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.2fr)_minmax(340px,1fr)]",
            )}
            data-stacked={isWideLauncherStage ? "true" : undefined}
          >
            {/* The bay itself stays mounted and only its contents change. This
                makes LR/RC a true in-place toggle, while Today remains the
                unchanged live-status sibling beside it. */}
            <section
              data-section={section}
              className="drill-observatory-practice-bay"
              aria-label={
                stage === "default" ? "Practice and insight workspace" : "Drill launcher"
              }
              data-tour={stage === "default" ? "mainhub-practice" : "mainhub-launcher"}
            >
              {isLauncherStage ? (
                <LauncherStage
                  kind={stage}
                  section={section}
                  drillReturnTo={drillReturnTo}
                  onCancel={closeStage}
                />
              ) : (
                <PracticeInsightStage
                  section={section}
                  onOpenAnalytics={openAnalytics}
                  onOpenLauncher={openLauncher}
                  canOpenAnalytics={canOpenAnalytics}
                  canOpenJournal={canOpenJournal}
                />
              )}
            </section>

            <section className="drill-observatory-today-bay">
              <TodayFocusPanel drillReturnTo={drillReturnTo} />
            </section>
          </section>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

.drill-observatory-root {
  color: var(--observatory-ink);
  font-family: "Outfit", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
}

.drill-observatory-canvas {
  isolation: isolate;
}

/* ── Shared micro-typography ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Tailwind's `font-mono` stack does not include the label face, so anything
   using it landed on ui-monospace — a different face from the labels beside it.
   The Today panel's date sat directly next to "TODAY" in two different monos. */
.drill-observatory-root .font-mono {
  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font);
}

/* One treatment for every uppercase micro-label in the observatory. Applied via
   an explicit class rather than a blanket `.uppercase` selector — the
   observatory hosts launcher stages whose labels are not part of this set. */
.drill-observatory-label {
  color: var(--observatory-ink-42);
  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font) !important;
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-label) !important;
  font-weight: 500 !important;
  letter-spacing: var(--observatory-track-label) !important;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.drill-observatory-label-sm {
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-label-sm) !important;
}

/* The ink ladder, addressable from JSX. Tailwind arbitrary values would work but
   would scatter rgba(244,241,234,x) literals across three components and detach
   them from the token block above; these keep one source for the scale. */
.drill-observatory-ink-full { color: var(--observatory-ink) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-90 { color: var(--observatory-ink-90) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-85 { color: var(--observatory-ink-85) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-70 { color: var(--observatory-ink-70) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-62 { color: var(--observatory-ink-62) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-60 { color: var(--observatory-ink-60) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-55 { color: var(--observatory-ink-55) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-50 { color: var(--observatory-ink-50) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-48 { color: var(--observatory-ink-48) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-45 { color: var(--observatory-ink-45) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-42 { color: var(--observatory-ink-42) !important; }
.drill-observatory-ink-38 { color: var(--observatory-ink-38) !important; }

/* ── Hero ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

.drill-observatory-hero {
  background-color: var(--observatory-surface-image) !important;
  background-image: url("/mainhub/hub-horizon.png");
  /* WebP is 37KB against the PNG's 384KB; the PNG line above stays as the
     fallback for anything that cannot parse image-set. */
  background-image: image-set(
    url("/mainhub/hub-horizon.webp") type("image/webp"),
    url("/mainhub/hub-horizon.png") type("image/png")
  );
  background-position: center 68%;
  background-repeat: no-repeat;
  background-size: cover;
  border: 0 !important;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-shell) !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring),
    var(--observatory-shadow-image) !important;
  color: var(--observatory-ink);
  min-height: 0 !important;
  overflow: hidden;
  position: relative;
}

/* Scrim. Mandatory, not decorative: the greeting sits over the image's bright
   terminator at some crops and is unreadable without it. */
.drill-observatory-hero::before {
  background:
    linear-gradient(
      90deg,
      rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.82) 0%,
      rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.35) 45%,
      rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.7) 100%
    ),
    linear-gradient(
      180deg,
      rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.5),
      transparent 30%,
      transparent 70%,
      rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.55)
    );
  content: "";
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 0;
}

/* The Question jump's band on the Drill landing workspace. Full width, matching
   Classroom Home: three dropdowns right-aligned against the canvas edge left a
   ragged gutter beside the hero's left column, and the row is navigation for
   the whole surface rather than a control belonging to its right side. The
   canvas gap supplies the spacing below it, so this row contributes nothing but
   its own height. */
/* The tools row carries two controls now: QuestionJump on the left, the
   LR/RC section switch hard right. It was `display: block` while it held only
   the one. */
.drill-observatory-tools {
  align-items: center;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 12px;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: -4px;
}

/* The address row takes the space that is left; the search trigger and the
   switch keep their intrinsic width and stay together on one line, wrapping as
   a pair rather than one above the other. */
.drill-observatory-tools > .qj {
  flex: 1 1 320px;
  width: auto;
}

.drill-observatory-tools-aside {
  align-items: center;
  display: flex;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-left: auto;
}

.drill-observatory-tools-aside .drill-observatory-section-toggle {
  margin-left: 0;
}

/* ── Section switch (LR | RC) ────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Note the surface it sits on: the observatory is dark CARDS on a light cream
   stage, and the tools row is on the stage, not on a card. So the switch is
   inked dark-on-light and takes its values from the Question finder beside it
   (questionJump.css light tone) — the two are one utility strip and must not
   read as two different systems. An ivory-on-ivory switch, which the first
   pass shipped, was invisible here. */
.drill-observatory-section-toggle {
  align-items: center;
  background: #fbf8f3;
  border-radius: 999px;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.09);
  display: inline-flex;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  gap: 2px;
  margin-left: auto;
  padding: 3px;
}

.drill-observatory-section-toggle-option {
  background: none;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: #a7a29f;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font);
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-label);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: var(--observatory-track-route);
  line-height: 1;
  padding: 7px 16px;
  position: relative;
  transition: color 200ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 0.68, 0.24, 1);
}

.drill-observatory-section-toggle-option:hover {
  color: #332f2c;
}

.drill-observatory-section-toggle-option.is-active {
  color: #f5f1ec;
}

.drill-observatory-section-toggle-option:focus-visible {
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(51, 47, 44, 0.35);
  outline: none;
}

/* The travelling lit slot. Sits behind the label — the label carries
   `position: relative` and a z-index of its own so it is never covered. */
.drill-observatory-section-toggle-indicator {
  background: #332f2c;
  border-radius: 999px;
  inset: 0;
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 0;
}

@media (max-width: 719px) {
  /* Narrow: the switch drops under QuestionJump at full width rather than
     shrinking into two unreadable slots. It is never hidden — unlike the route
     label's trailing span, which is decoration. */
  .drill-observatory-section-toggle {
    justify-content: center;
    margin-left: 0;
    width: 100%;
  }

  /* Except when it is riding in the aside beside the search trigger, where
     100% is 100% of a box that already holds the trigger — the switch grew
     past its own container and off the right edge of the screen. In the
     cluster it takes its natural width; the trigger has collapsed to its icon
     by here, so the pair fits on one line. */
  .drill-observatory-tools-aside .drill-observatory-section-toggle {
    width: auto;
  }

  .drill-observatory-section-toggle-option {
    flex: 1 1 0;
  }
}

.drill-observatory-console {

/* Radix puts data-placeholder on the TRIGGER, not on the value span — the

.drill-observatory-console-start {

.drill-observatory-advanced,
.drill-observatory-search-results {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28) !important;

/* ── Lower deck ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Two separate cards with a real gap, not one shell split by a divider. The
   practice card is flat black; Today carries the eclipse. */
.drill-observatory-deck {

  border-radius: 0;

/* Tools are a hairline split, not two boxed cards. */
/* One column per tool. This was 1fr 1fr and stayed that way when Reading

   under a hole. auto-fit keeps it honest as tools are added or removed. */
.drill-observatory-practice-tools {
  border: 0;
  display: grid;
  grid-auto-flow: column;
  grid-auto-columns: 1fr;
}

.drill-observatory-practice-tile {

  border: 0 !important;

  gap: 10px !important;
  padding: 2px 24px 2px 2px !important;

}

.drill-observatory-practice-tile + .drill-observatory-practice-tile {

}

.drill-observatory-practice-tile:hover {

.drill-observatory-practice-tile:active {

}

/* Reading Comprehension turns the tool row over instead of navigating away.
   The faces are absolutely stacked and the deck's height is set from JS, so
   the container has to be positioned and its height transitioned here. */
.drill-observatory-flip {
  perspective: 1400px;
  position: relative;
  transition: height 420ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 0.68, 0.24, 1);
}

.drill-observatory-flip-face {
  backface-visibility: hidden;

  top: 0;

}

.drill-observatory-flip-front {

}

.drill-observatory-flip-back {
  transform: rotateY(-180deg);
}

/* visibility (not opacity) so the hidden face leaves the tab order — a

.drill-observatory-flip-face[aria-hidden="true"] {
  visibility: hidden;
}

.drill-observatory-flip.is-flipped .drill-observatory-flip-front {
  transform: rotateY(180deg);
}

.drill-observatory-flip.is-flipped .drill-observatory-flip-back {

}

import * as React from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  BarChart3,
  BookOpen,
  Grid3x3,
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Pause,
  Play,
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useStudentAnalytics } from "@/hooks/useStudentAnalytics";
import { buildInsightSlides } from "@/components/mainhub/insightSlides";
import type { DrillSection } from "@/lib/drillSection";
import { RcSessionHistory } from "@/features/rc/components/RcSessionHistory";
import "@/features/rc/rc.css";

const ROTATE_MS = 7000;

interface PracticeInsightStageProps {
  /**
   * Which bank the hub is on. The bay is shared by both sections now, so the
   * two tiles name the bank they open: "a targeted question set" was
   * describing a passage builder on the RC side.
   */
  section?: DrillSection;
  /** Opens the embedded analytics stage at the given tab. */
  onOpenAnalytics: (tab?: string) => void;
  /** Opens an embedded drill-launcher stage (was the /practice hub). */
  onOpenLauncher: (kind: "type-drill") => void;
  canOpenAnalytics: boolean;
  canOpenJournal: boolean;
}

/**
 * Replaces the orbital ring. A stable pair of primary practice actions
 * (Full Section / Build a Set) plus an auto-rotating, real-data insight
 * carousel. Dark/glass to sit as a sibling to the hero and Today panel.
 */
export default function PracticeInsightStage({
  section = "lr",
  onOpenAnalytics,
  onOpenLauncher,
  canOpenAnalytics,
  canOpenJournal,
}: PracticeInsightStageProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { analytics, streak, loading } = useStudentAnalytics();
  const slides = React.useMemo(
    () => buildInsightSlides(analytics, streak),
    [analytics, streak],
  );

  const [index, setIndex] = React.useState(0);
  const [paused, setPaused] = React.useState(false);
  const [userPaused, setUserPaused] = React.useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    // RC does not draw the carousel, so nothing should be rotating behind it.
    if (section === "rc" || paused || userPaused || slides.length <= 1) return;
    const t = window.setInterval(
      () => setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % slides.length),
      ROTATE_MS,
    );
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [section, paused, userPaused, slides.length]);

  const slide = slides[index] ?? slides[0];

  const handleSlideAction = React.useCallback(
    (href: string, tab: string) => {
      if (href.startsWith("/classroom/drill?stage=analytics")) {
        onOpenAnalytics(tab);
        return;
      }
      if (href.startsWith("/classroom/drill?stage=type-drill")) {
        onOpenLauncher("type-drill");
        return;
      }
      navigate(href);
    },
    [navigate, onOpenAnalytics, onOpenLauncher],
  );

  return (
    /* Surface, padding and the hairline split live in .drill-observatory-practice
       and -practice-tools. Flat #08080b: no texture, no wash, no grid. */
    <div className="drill-observatory-practice relative flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
      {/* Practice Tools */}
      <div>
        <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-42 mb-4">
          Practice tools
        </p>
        {/* Section choice belongs to the LR/RC switch above. Practice tools
            only describe what the student can do inside the active section. */}
        <div className="drill-observatory-practice-tools">
          <PracticeTile
            icon={Grid3x3}
            title="Build a Set"
            description={
              section === "rc"
                ? "Choose passages by topic, format, and difficulty."
                : "Choose questions by type, reasoning, and level."
            }
            onClick={() => onOpenLauncher("type-drill")}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-[18px] flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {canOpenAnalytics && (
            <SecondaryLink icon={BarChart3} label="Analytics" onClick={() => onOpenAnalytics("overview")} />
          )}
          {canOpenJournal && (
            <SecondaryLink icon={BookOpen} label="Journal" onClick={() => navigate("/journal")} />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Rotating insight preview — LR only. The carousel is built from LR
          attempts, so on RC it would be one section's numbers presented as the
          other's. RC's own drill history takes the slot instead: it is the
          real, RC-specific status, and it had nowhere to live once the two
          bays became one. RcHubSurface still skips drawing a history of its
          own on the assumption that this slot carries it. */}
      {section === "rc" ? (
        <div className="drill-observatory-insight-section relative">
          <div className="mb-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
            <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-42">
              Your reading drills
            </p>
          </div>
          <RcSessionHistory tone="dark" />
        </div>
      ) : (
      <div
        className="drill-observatory-insight-section relative"
        onMouseEnter={() => setPaused(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setPaused(false)}
      >
        <div className="mb-3.5 flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-42">
            Insight preview
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" role="tablist" aria-label="Insight slides">
              {slides.map((s, i) => (
                <button
                  key={s.id}
                  type="button"
                  role="tab"
                  aria-label={s.kicker}
                  aria-selected={i === index}
                  onClick={() => setIndex(i)}
                  /* The dot stays 6px; the *target* is 24px. A centred
                     pseudo-element carries the hit area so the control meets
                     the 24x24 minimum without the dots visually drifting
                     apart. */
                  className={cn(
                    "relative h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all",
                    "before:absolute before:left-1/2 before:top-1/2 before:h-6 before:w-6",
                    "before:-translate-x-1/2 before:-translate-y-1/2 before:content-['']",
                    i === index
                      ? "w-5 bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.75)]"
                      : "w-1.5 bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.22)] hover:bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.45)]",
                  )}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
            {slides.length > 1 && (
              /* Auto-rotating content needs a real stop, not just
                 pause-on-hover: hover reaches neither keyboard nor touch. */
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setUserPaused((p) => !p)}
                aria-label={
                  userPaused ? "Resume insight rotation" : "Pause insight rotation"
                }
                aria-pressed={userPaused}
                className="drill-observatory-ink-38 grid h-6 w-6 place-items-center rounded-full transition hover:bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.08)]"
              >
                {userPaused ? (
                  <Play className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />
                ) : (
                  <Pause className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />
                )}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="drill-observatory-insight relative">
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {slide && (
              <motion.div
                key={slide.id}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.28 }}
                className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-5"
              >
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-38 block">
                    {slide.kicker}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={cn(
                      "drill-observatory-insight-value mt-[7px] block",
                      slide.empty && "drill-observatory-ink-55",
                    )}
                  >
                    {loading ? "…" : slide.headline}
                  </span>
                  <span className="drill-observatory-ink-48 mt-1 block text-[12.5px] font-normal leading-[1.45]">
                    {slide.detail}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleSlideAction(slide.actionHref, slide.tab)}
                  className="drill-observatory-insight-action inline-flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  {slide.actionLabel}
                  <ArrowRight className="h-[13px] w-[13px]" strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden />
                </button>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


export function PracticeTile({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  onClick,
}: {
  icon: React.ElementType;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={cn(
        "drill-observatory-practice-tile",
        "group flex flex-col items-start text-left",
      )}
    >
      <Icon
        className="drill-observatory-ink-85 h-5 w-5"
        strokeWidth={1.8}
        aria-hidden
      />
      <div>
        <div className="drill-observatory-ink-full text-[15px] font-semibold">{title}</div>
        <div className="drill-observatory-ink-48 mt-[3px] text-[12.5px] leading-[1.45]">
          {description}
        </div>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}

export function SecondaryLink({
  icon: Icon,
  label,
  onClick,
}: {
  icon: React.ElementType;
  label: string;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className="drill-observatory-secondary-link inline-flex items-center"
    >
      <Icon className="h-[13px] w-[13px]" strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden />
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { DRILL_SECTION_LABELS, type DrillSection } from "@/lib/drillSection";

const ORDER: DrillSection[] = ["lr", "rc"];

/**
 * The drill hub's section switch: LR on the left, RC on the right, top-right of
 * the tools row.
 *
 * A flip switch, not a tab strip. Both labels stay visible and a single lit
 * indicator travels between them — `layoutId` so the browser moves one object
 * rather than cross-fading two, which is what makes it read as a switch being
 * thrown. The bands below flip with it (see `.drill-observatory-section-flip`).
 *
 * Switching is unconditional and silent: no confirm, even when the LR picker
 * holds a selection that the switch discards. A flip that stops to ask is not
 * a flip.
 */
export function SectionToggle({
  section,
  onChange,
  className,
}: {
  section: DrillSection;
  onChange: (next: DrillSection) => void;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const refs = React.useRef<Partial<Record<DrillSection, HTMLButtonElement | null>>>({});

  // Arrow keys move between the two options and activate as they go, the way a
  // radio group does — the switch has exactly two states, so "move focus, then
  // press" would be two actions for a control that exists to take one.
  const onKeyDown = React.useCallback(
    (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
      if (event.key !== "ArrowLeft" && event.key !== "ArrowRight") return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const index = ORDER.indexOf(section);
      const next = ORDER[(index + (event.key === "ArrowRight" ? 1 : ORDER.length - 1)) % ORDER.length];
      onChange(next);
      refs.current[next]?.focus();
    },
    [onChange, section],
  );

  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label="Drill section"
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      className={cn("drill-observatory-section-toggle", className)}
    >
      {ORDER.map((id) => {
        const active = id === section;
        return (
          <button
            key={id}
            ref={(node) => {
              refs.current[id] = node;
            }}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={active}
            aria-label={DRILL_SECTION_LABELS[id].full}
            title={DRILL_SECTION_LABELS[id].full}
            // Only the active option is in the tab order: one Tab stop lands on
            // the switch, and the arrows work it from there.
            tabIndex={active ? 0 : -1}
            onClick={() => onChange(id)}
            className={cn(
              "drill-observatory-section-toggle-option",
              active && "is-active",
            )}
          >
            {active && (
              <motion.span
                layoutId="drill-section-indicator"
                className="drill-observatory-section-toggle-indicator"
                aria-hidden
                transition={
                  reduceMotion
                    ? { duration: 0 }
                    : { type: "spring", stiffness: 420, damping: 34, mass: 0.7 }
                }
              />
            )}
            <span className="relative z-[1]">{DRILL_SECTION_LABELS[id].short}</span>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export default SectionToggle;

/* The per-type gradient tone map is gone with the 2026-08-01 reskin. Six near-

function compactText(text: string | null | undefined, max = 92) {
  const value = text?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  if (!value) return null;
  return value.length > max ? `${value.slice(0, max - 3)}...` : value;
}

function dueLabel(dueAt: string | null | undefined) {
  if (!dueAt) return null;
  const date = new Date(dueAt);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;
  return isToday(date) ? "Due today" : `Due ${format(date, "MMM d")}`;
}

function statusOrder(status?: string) {
  if (status === "In progress") return 0;
  if (status === "Ready") return 1;
  if (status === "Done") return 3;
  return 2;
}

function itemSort(a: TodayFocusItem, b: TodayFocusItem) {
  const priorityDelta = b.priority - a.priority;
  if (priorityDelta !== 0) return priorityDelta;

.drill-observatory-secondary-link {
  background: transparent !important;
  border: 1px solid var(--observatory-ring-glass) !important;
  border-radius: 999px !important;
  color: var(--observatory-ink-70) !important;
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-meta) !important;
  font-weight: 500 !important;
  gap: 7px;
  padding: 8px 14px !important;
  transition: border-color var(--observatory-motion), color var(--observatory-motion),
    transform var(--observatory-motion);
}

.drill-observatory-secondary-link:hover {
  border-color: var(--observatory-ring-strong) !important;
  color: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.95) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-secondary-link:active {
  transform: translateY(0.5px);
}

/* Insight preview is a section under a hairline, not a nested card. */
.drill-observatory-insight-section {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--observatory-ring-quiet);
  padding-top: var(--observatory-space-5);
}

.drill-observatory-insight {
  background: transparent !important;
  border: 0 !important;
  border-radius: 0 !important;
  backdrop-filter: none !important;
  min-height: 84px !important;
  padding: 0 !important;
}

.drill-observatory-insight-value {
  color: var(--observatory-ink-90);
  font-family: var(--observatory-display-font);
  font-size: 22px;
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.25;
}

.drill-observatory-insight-action {
  background: transparent !important;
  border: 0 !important;
  border-radius: 999px !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.16) !important;
  color: var(--observatory-ink-90) !important;
  font-size: 12.5px !important;
  font-weight: 500 !important;
  padding: 11px 18px !important;
  transition: box-shadow var(--observatory-motion), transform var(--observatory-motion);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.drill-observatory-insight-action:hover {
  background: transparent !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.3) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-insight-action:active {
  transform: translateY(0.5px);
}

/* ── Today ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

.drill-observatory-today {
  background: none !important;
  border-radius: 0 !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 0 !important;
}

.drill-observatory-today-inner {
  background-color: var(--observatory-surface-image) !important;
  background-image: url("/mainhub/hub-eclipse.png");
  background-image: image-set(
    url("/mainhub/hub-eclipse.webp") type("image/webp"),
    url("/mainhub/hub-eclipse.png") type("image/png")
  );
  background-position: 82% 30%;
  background-repeat: no-repeat;
  background-size: 160% auto;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-shell) !important;
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring),
    var(--observatory-shadow-image) !important;
  gap: var(--observatory-space-3);
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: var(--observatory-space-6) !important;
  position: relative;
}

.drill-observatory-today-inner::before {
  background: linear-gradient(
    180deg,
    rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.55),
    rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.25) 40%,
    rgba(4, 4, 6, 0.75)
  );
  content: "";
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  position: absolute;
  z-index: 0;
}

.drill-observatory-today-primary {
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  background: var(--observatory-glass-soft) !important;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-inner) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring) !important;
  min-height: 0 !important;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 0 !important;
}

.drill-observatory-today-primary > div {
  background: none !important;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-inner) !important;
  min-height: 0 !important;
}

.drill-observatory-today-title {
  color: var(--observatory-ink);
  font-family: var(--observatory-display-font);
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-heading);
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.3;
}

.drill-observatory-today-chip {
  border: 1px solid var(--observatory-ring-chip);
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--observatory-ink-85);
  font-family: var(--observatory-label-font);
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-label-sm);
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: var(--observatory-track-chip);
  padding: 5px 10px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.drill-observatory-today-action {
  border-top: 1px solid var(--observatory-ring);
  transition: background-color var(--observatory-motion);
}

.drill-observatory-today-action:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background-color: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.05);
}

.drill-observatory-today-rail {
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  background: var(--observatory-glass-row) !important;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-control) !important;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring-quiet) !important;
  padding: var(--observatory-space-3) var(--observatory-space-4) !important;
  transition: transform var(--observatory-motion), box-shadow var(--observatory-motion);
}

.drill-observatory-today-rail:hover {
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring-glass),
    0 10px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28) !important;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.drill-observatory-today-rail:active {
  transform: translateY(0.5px);
}

.drill-observatory-today-quiet {
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  background: var(--observatory-glass-row);
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-control);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-ring-quiet);
  color: var(--observatory-ink-45);
  font-size: var(--observatory-text-meta);
  padding: var(--observatory-space-3) var(--observatory-space-4);
}

/* ── Analytics stage ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* AnalyticsStage is written against the app's light-only shadcn tokens; dropped
   on this surface it renders grey-on-black. Re-map it rather than fork it. */
.drill-observatory-analytics {
  background: var(--observatory-surface-card);
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-shell);
  box-shadow:
    0 0 0 1px var(--observatory-hairline),
    var(--observatory-shadow-card);
  color: var(--observatory-ink);
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 26px 28px;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .bg-white,
.drill-observatory-analytics .bg-slate-100 {
  background-color: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.035) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .border-slate-200,
.drill-observatory-analytics .border-slate-300,
.drill-observatory-analytics .border-slate-100 {
  border-color: var(--observatory-hairline) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-900,
.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-800,
.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-700,
.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-600 {
  color: var(--observatory-ink) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-500,
.drill-observatory-analytics .text-slate-400 {
  color: var(--observatory-ink-55) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .shadow-sm,
.drill-observatory-analytics .shadow {
  box-shadow: none !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .rounded-xl,
.drill-observatory-analytics .rounded-2xl {
  border-radius: var(--observatory-radius-control) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics table tr {
  border-color: var(--observatory-hairline) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .bg-slate-900 {
  background-color: var(--observatory-ink) !important;
  color: #0c0c0e !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .bg-rose-50 {
  background-color: rgba(240, 106, 118, 0.12) !important;
}

.drill-observatory-analytics .text-rose-600 {
  color: #ff97a1 !important;
}

/* ── Responsive ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* The console is a fixed 392px by design. Below ~1080px of canvas the hero's
   left column starts losing the greeting, so the console narrows before the
   layout stacks. */
@media (max-width: 1199px) {
  .drill-observatory-hero-grid {
    gap: var(--observatory-space-5) !important;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 356px !important;
    padding: var(--observatory-space-2) var(--observatory-space-6) var(--observatory-space-6) !important;
  }

}

@media (max-width: 1023px) {
  .classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-stage {
    padding: var(--observatory-space-4) var(--observatory-space-5) var(--observatory-space-5);
  }

}

@media (max-width: 899px) {
  .drill-observatory-hero-grid {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) !important;
    grid-template-rows: auto auto !important;
    min-height: 0 !important;
    padding-top: var(--observatory-space-5) !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-hero {
    background-position: center 78%;
  }

  .drill-observatory-greeting {
    align-self: start;
    max-width: none;
  }

  .drill-observatory-console {
    align-self: stretch;
    max-width: 460px !important;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 759px) {
  .classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-stage {
    padding: var(--observatory-space-3) var(--observatory-space-4) var(--observatory-space-5);
  }

  .drill-observatory-hero-grid {
    gap: var(--observatory-space-5) !important;
    padding: var(--observatory-space-4) !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-console {
    max-width: none !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-practice,
  .drill-observatory-launcher,
  .drill-observatory-today-inner,
  .drill-observatory-analytics {
    padding: var(--observatory-space-5) !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-practice-tools {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  }

  .drill-observatory-practice-tile {
    padding: 2px 2px 14px !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-practice-tile + .drill-observatory-practice-tile {
    border-left: 0 !important;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--observatory-hairline) !important;
    padding: 14px 2px 2px !important;
  }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .drill-observatory-root *,
  .drill-observatory-root *::before,
  .drill-observatory-root *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
  }

  .drill-observatory-practice-tile:hover,
  .drill-observatory-today-rail:hover {
    transform: none;
  }

}

.classroom-root .classroom-section-trigger {
  align-items: center;
  background: var(--classroom-trigger);
  border: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);
  border-radius: var(--classroom-radius);
  cursor: pointer;
  display: none;
  font-size: 14px;
  height: 48px;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 32px;
  padding: 0 16px;
  width: 100%;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-dateline {
  align-items: center;
  color: var(--classroom-meta-soft);
  display: flex;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 500;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-title {
  font-size: 30px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  line-height: 1.15;
  margin: 0 0 24px;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-message {
  border: 1px solid var(--classroom-line);
  border-radius: var(--classroom-radius);
  margin-bottom: 48px;
  padding: 20px;
  transition: border-color var(--classroom-transition) ease;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-message header {
  align-items: center;
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

/* ── the question jump row ────────────────────────────────────────────────
 *
 * One band, four controls: three address selects and the text-search trigger.
 * It inherits the finder's tokens (questionFinder.css defines them on `.qf`,
 * and `.qj` repeats the same set) so the two never drift apart visually — they
 * are one control surface split across an inline row and a panel.
 *
 * Rank: this sits under the masthead and above the hero, and must read as a
 * utility strip rather than a headline. Hence the sunk surface, the hairline
 * rather than a border, and no shadow of its own — it recedes, the hero stays
 * the loudest thing on the page. */

.qj {
  --qj-ink: #332f2c;
  --qj-muted: #a7a29f;
  --qj-sunk: #fbf8f3;
  --qj-hair: rgba(68, 64, 61, 0.09);
  --qj-ui: "Outfit", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;

  width: 100%;
}

.qj[data-tone="dark"] {
  --qj-ink: #f5f1ec;
  --qj-muted: #8b837b;
  --qj-sunk: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
  --qj-hair: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.qj-row {
  align-items: end;
  background: var(--qj-sunk);
  border-radius: 14px;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--qj-hair);
  display: grid;
  /* The three addresses share equally; search takes only what its trigger
     needs. A fourth equal column made the search button stretch into a slab
     the width of a dropdown, which read as a fourth step in the sequence. */
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)) auto;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
}

/* Without the search trigger the row is three addresses and nothing else, so
   the reserved `auto` column would leave a dead gutter on the right. */
.qj-row[data-search="false"] {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
}

.qj-field {
  min-width: 0;
}

/* The trigger is a control, not a field, so it aligns to the selects' baseline
   rather than to the label row above them. */
.qj-search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  padding-bottom: 1px;
}

/* Phones: the addresses stay a row of three — breaking them into a column
   turns a three-second jump into a scroll — and search drops beneath. */
@media (max-width: 600px) {
  .qj-row {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
    row-gap: 12px;
  }

  .qj-search {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
    padding-bottom: 0;
  }

  .qj-search .qf,
  .qj-search .qf-trigger {
    width: 100%;
  }
}
