{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\wiki\\DRILL-HUB-IMPECCABLE-AUDIT-2026-09-08.md", "content": "# Drill hub: impeccable audit and critique (2026-09-08)\n\nTarget: `/classroom/drill` on production (lsatprep.study), signed in as Joshua. LR section, default stage, the Build a Set launcher stage, desktop (~740px pane) and phone (375px). Source: `src/pages/AcademyMainhub.tsx`, `src/components/mainhub/PracticeInsightStage.tsx`, `src/components/mainhub/TodayFocusPanel.tsx`, `src/components/drill/SectionToggle.tsx`, `src/components/questionfinder/QuestionJump.tsx`, `src/components/drill/QuestionPicker.tsx`, and the `.drill-observatory-*` block of `src/components/classroom/classroom.css`.\n\nRegister: product. Every number below was measured in the live DOM, not read off the token file. Related: [[COPY-CORRECTIONS]], [[teaching-voice]].\n\n---\n\n## Part 1: Technical audit\n\n### Audit Health Score\n\n| # | Dimension | Score | Key finding |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Accessibility | 1 | Question-jump labels render at 1.07:1; hero CTA has no focus ring; most micro-labels sit below 4.5:1 |\n| 2 | Performance | 3 | Four backdrop-filter surfaces and one height transition; images are lean (37 KB + 13 KB WebP) |\n| 3 | Responsive | 2 | Today rails overflow 57px past a 375px viewport; fixed menu button covers content under 1024px |\n| 4 | Theming | 2 | Tokens exist but JSX carries literal rgba/hex; the hub ignores the theme toggle; 206 `!important` in the file |\n| 5 | Anti-patterns | 2 | Glass on four surfaces, 60px shadows, three type families, a rotating metric carousel |\n| **Total** | | **10/20** | **Acceptable, significant work needed** |\n\n### Anti-patterns verdict\n\nPartial pass. Nobody would say \"AI made this\" at first glance: the horizon photo is real, the copy says what each button does, and the switch is a switch rather than tabs. But the recipe underneath is the 2025 \"premium dark dashboard\" lane: serif display over a space photo, uppercase mono micro-labels tracked at 0.18em, glass panels with hairline rings, a pill CTA, and an auto-rotating insight carousel with dots. Four surfaces use `backdrop-filter` (console, Today primary, Today rails, Today quiet). Card shadows run to `0 24px 60px`. DESIGN.md names all three of those as defects in the Workroom and reserves the serif for the Hall. Either this hub is a sanctioned scoped world and needs its own surface brief under `.impeccable/surfaces/`, or it is a seam leak. Today it is undocumented, which makes it the latter by default.\n\n### Executive summary\n\n- Score 10/20.\n- Issues: P0 1, P1 7, P2 7, P3 4.\n- Top five:\n  1. The Test / Section / Question row is inked ivory on the cream stage. Labels measure 1.07:1. It is invisible.\n  2. The fixed \"Open classroom menu\" button sits over page content on every viewport under 1024px, covering the launcher title and the \"Practice tools\" label.\n  3. The primary CTA (Start Smart Drill) shows no focus indicator when focused.\n  4. On a phone, the Today rails render 432px wide inside a 375px viewport and truncate assignment titles.\n  5. The observatory ink ladder puts every label at or below `ink-48` under 4.5:1 on the card black. That is most of the page's labels.\n\n### Detailed findings\n\n**[P0] Question-jump row is invisible on the stage**\n- Location: `src/pages/AcademyMainhub.tsx:400` passes `tone=\"dark\"` to `QuestionJump`; `questionJump.css:23` sets `--qj-ink: #f5f1ec` for that tone.\n- Category: Accessibility, Theming.\n- Impact: \"Test\", \"Section\", \"Question\" and the select placeholders render at 1.07:1 on `#fbf8f3`. A student cannot see the fastest route to a specific question. The CSS comment on `.drill-observatory-section-toggle` already diagnoses this exact bug for the switch and fixes it there only.\n- WCAG: 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum).\n- Fix: pass `tone=\"light\"` for QuestionJump (and check QuestionFinder's trigger, which happens to look right only because its trigger is a dark pill). Verify in browser.\n- Command: `$impeccable harden`\n\n**[P1] Fixed menu trigger overlaps content below 1024px**\n- Location: `classroom.css:2295` (`.classroom-mobile-trigger`, `position: fixed; top: 16px; left: 16px; z-index: 70`). The drill stage has no top offset to clear it.\n- Impact: covers the \"Set up an LR drill\" launcher title, the \"Practice tools\" label, and the first select of the jump row. The button is also 38x38, under the 44px floor.\n- WCAG: 2.4.7 area, 2.5.8 target size.\n- Fix: reserve a 56px top inset on the drill stage under 1024px, or move the trigger into the tools row as a flow item. Size it 44x44.\n- Command: `$impeccable adapt`\n\n**[P1] Primary CTA has no focus indicator**\n- Location: `.drill-observatory-cta` in classroom.css sets `border: 0 !important` and only a shadow; measured `outline: none` while focused.\n- Impact: keyboard users lose the page's most important control.\n- WCAG: 2.4.7 Focus Visible.\n- Fix: add `:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--observatory-ink); outline-offset: 3px }`. Same check for `.drill-observatory-console-start`.\n- Command: `$impeccable harden`\n\n**[P1] Phone: Today rails overflow the viewport**\n- Location: `TodayFocusPanel.tsx:585` rail rows; the rail's fixed label span and `truncate` children compute to 432px right edge in a 375px viewport. `.drill-observatory-today-inner` clips it with `overflow: hidden`, so titles are cut (\"New assignment: Necessary Assumption - First To\").\n- Impact: the most important computed next step is unreadable on the device students check between classes.\n- Fix: give the rail `min-width: 0` on every flex child and let the trailing label wrap or drop under 400px.\n- Command: `$impeccable adapt`\n\n**[P1] Micro-label contrast across the hub**\n- Location: `--observatory-ink-38/42/45/48` (classroom.css:874-877) used by \"Practice tools\", \"Insight preview\", \"Top priority\", slide details, tile descriptions.\n- Impact: measured 3.22:1 to 4.2:1 at 10 to 12.5px. Below AA for normal text. PRODUCT.md calls AA a defect gate.\n- WCAG: 1.4.3.\n- Fix: floor the ladder at `ink-55` (4.9:1) for any text; keep the lower steps for decoration only.\n- Command: `$impeccable colorize` (contrast pass), then `$impeccable polish`\n\n**[P1] Inactive switch option and jump selects fail contrast and size**\n- Location: `.drill-observatory-section-toggle-option` `#a7a29f` on `#fbf8f3` = 2.38:1; option box 49x25px. Jump selects 94x39px.\n- Fix: darken inactive to `#6b6663` or above; pad options to 44px tall; selects to 44px.\n- Command: `$impeccable harden`\n\n**[P1] Em-dash in student-facing placeholder**\n- Location: `QuestionPicker.tsx:897` placeholder \"Add exact questions — PT101.S2.Q1, PT101.S2.Q6\".\n- Impact: PRODUCT.md brand commitment, binding on utility copy.\n- Fix: \"Add exact questions, e.g. PT101.S2.Q1, PT101.S2.Q6\".\n- Command: `$impeccable clarify`\n\n**[P1] Small targets throughout**\n- Location: carousel dots 6x6 (24px hit area via pseudo-element), pause 24x24, Analytics/Journal pills 37px tall, \"Find a question\" 42px, \"Build targeted set\" 42px.\n- Fix: 44px minimum by height, per DESIGN.md button rule.\n- Command: `$impeccable harden`\n\n**[P2] No h1, and headings out of order**\n- Page outline: h2 \"Welcome back\", h3 \"Build the drill you need.\", h2 (assignment title inside Today). \"Practice tools\", \"Today\", \"Insight preview\" are paragraphs.\n- Fix: h1 for the greeting, h2 for each bay's label, h3 for items.\n- Command: `$impeccable harden`\n\n**[P2] Glass and shadow against the design record**\n- `backdrop-filter: blur()` on four classes, `0 24px 60px` shadows on hero, cards and Today. DESIGN.md: no shadows, no glass in the Workroom.\n- Fix: decide the hub's status (see critique) and either write its surface brief or flatten.\n- Command: `$impeccable document` (brief) or `$impeccable quieter`\n\n**[P2] Height transition on the RC flip**\n- `classroom.css:1486` `.drill-observatory-flip { transition: height }`. Layout property.\n- Fix: animate `transform`/`opacity` and let height snap, or use `grid-template-rows` interpolation.\n- Command: `$impeccable animate`\n\n**[P2] Literal colours in JSX**\n- `PracticeInsightStage.tsx` carousel dots and pause hover use `bg-[rgba(244,241,234,…)]`; `TodayFocusPanel.tsx` focus rings and skeletons likewise; `AcademyMainhub.tsx:146` `text-[#f4f1ea]`; `SectionToggle` colours in CSS as hex. DESIGN.md's Named Ground Rule.\n- Command: `$impeccable extract`\n\n**[P2] Hub ignores the theme toggle**\n- The dark cards and cream stage are hard-coded; the app's dark alternate leaves the stage cream. Acceptable only if this is a scoped world with its own brief.\n- Command: `$impeccable document`\n\n**[P2] Two hero texts compete on the phone**\n- Under 900px the hero stacks greeting, then console, so the two CTAs sit 220px apart with the same weight. The console's paragraph wraps to five lines on 375px.\n- Command: `$impeccable adapt`\n\n**[P3] Date without year** while Classroom Home prints \"TUESDAY · SEPTEMBER 8 · 2026\". One format across both mounts.\n**[P3] \"Pick a test\" under Section and Question** is intentional (it points at the missing step) but reads as a copy error. \"Choose a test first\" would say why.\n**[P3] 5.5 MB question bank** loads before the LR launcher draws. Already documented in code; a skeleton of the filter frame would hide the wait.\n**[P3] 46 off-ramp font sizes** flagged by the detector (11.5, 12.5, 13.5, 15px). Harmless individually; they mean the observatory has its own type ramp that DESIGN.md does not know about.\n\n### Patterns and systemic issues\n\n- **The hub is a second design system.** Its own ink ladder, radius scale, spacing scale, type ramp, two extra families (Fraunces, JetBrains Mono), glass, shadows. None of it is in DESIGN.md or a surface brief. Every finding under Theming and Anti-patterns is a symptom of that.\n- **Tone props are set per component by hand.** The switch got fixed for the cream stage, the jump row did not. One `data-tone` on the tools row would have prevented it.\n- **Contrast decided by alpha, not measured.** A twelve-step alpha ladder invites picking by feel. Half the steps fail on the surface they were made for.\n- **`!important` as the layering strategy.** 206 in the file. The observatory overrides shadcn's Card and Button by force, which is why the shared focus ring never reaches the CTA.\n\n### Positive findings\n\n- The URL carries section and stage, so refresh, Back and shared links all land correctly. The replace-once trick for the bare URL is exactly right.\n- Copy is honest and specific: \"Start One Passage\" vs \"Start Smart Drill\" because the two do different things; the greeting paragraph says what the buttons do rather than \"Pick up where you left off\".\n- The carousel has a real pause control with `aria-pressed`, and rotation stops on RC where the data would lie. WCAG 2.2.2 is met.\n- Empty states are truthful: \"No streak yet. Answer a few questions today to start one.\"\n- The section switch is keyboard-correct (one tab stop, arrows move and activate, reduced motion honoured).\n- Images are WebP with a PNG fallback via `image-set`; hero 37 KB, eclipse 13 KB.\n- Reduced-motion block exists and stops the hover lifts.\n\n---\n\n## Part 2: Design critique\n\nAssessment A was a design read of source plus the live page. Assessment B was the impeccable detector: 103 findings over the seven files, 84 advisory (off-ramp sizes, radii, colours), and warnings for one 4px side-stripe (`classroom.css:2223`, outside the drill hub), nine \"overused font\" hits (seven Roboto Mono outside the hub, two Fraunces on Classroom Home), and two layout transitions (one is the hub's flip). The detector caught nothing the read missed inside the hub; its warnings mostly belong to other rooms in the same stylesheet. No browser overlay run: the `[Human]` overlay needs the live server injected into a page the pane can fully render, and the pane was hidden, so I read the DOM instead.\n\n### Design Health Score\n\n| # | Heuristic | Score | Key issue |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | Visibility of system status | 3 | Today is live and `aria-live`; the launcher's error state is a bare string |\n| 2 | Match system / real world | 3 | LSAT vocabulary is right; \"Smart Drill\" is the one label that does not say what it does |\n| 3 | User control and freedom | 3 | Cancel, deterministic Back, pause; the LR/RC flip silently discards a half-built set (by decision) |\n| 4 | Consistency and standards | 1 | Jump row wrong tone; date format differs from Home; three families against a one-face rule |\n| 5 | Error prevention | 3 | Address syntax has a hint and live count; \"Pick a test\" placeholder ambiguous |\n| 6 | Recognition rather than recall | 3 | Labels on everything; the carousel hides four of five insights at any moment |\n| 7 | Flexibility and efficiency | 3 | Cmd-K, arrow keys on the switch, typed addresses |\n| 8 | Aesthetic and minimalist design | 2 | Three doors to the same builder; hero stays above the open launcher |\n| 9 | Error recovery | 2 | Launcher error is one sentence with no retry |\n| 10 | Help and documentation | 2 | \"Faster than clicking\" is good; nothing explains what Smart Drill will do to you |\n| **Total** | | **25/40** | Needs work |\n\n### Overall impression\n\nIt is handsome and it is honest, and those are the two hard things. What it is not yet is decisive. PRODUCT.md's second principle says compute the next step, do not present a grid. This page presents, on one screen: a jump row, a search, a section switch, an adaptive CTA, a builder CTA, a builder tile, Analytics, Journal, an insight carousel with its own CTA, and then, last, the thing Joshua actually assigned. The computed next step is at the bottom right on desktop and fourth screen on a phone. The single biggest opportunity is inversion: lead with Today's \"Next up\", let the hero's two CTAs be the fallback for a student with nothing assigned.\n\n### What's working\n\n- **The switch.** One lit slot travelling between LR and RC, both labels visible, arrows work it. It reads as a physical control and the code says why.\n- **Copy that admits what it does.** \"Read one passage now, untimed, with the answers after it.\" No pitch, no metaphor, no coach voice invented to fill a gap.\n- **URL as the source of truth.** Section, stage, and the RC smart-start parameters all live in the address bar. That is why the page survives refresh and sharing.\n\n### Priority issues\n\n**[P0] The next step is buried.**\n- Why: a paying client logs in to do what Joshua assigned. Today's \"Next up\" sits below two hero CTAs, a tools card and a carousel. On a phone it is the fourth screen.\n- Fix: promote Today's primary item into the hero when one exists (\"Next up: Conditional Logic cheat sheet. Assigned by Joshua. Start\"). Demote Smart Drill and Build to the bay. With nothing assigned, today's hero is the fallback.\n- Command: `$impeccable shape`, then `$impeccable layout`\n\n**[P1] Three doors to one builder.**\n- Why: \"Set up an LR drill\" (hero console), \"Build a Set\" (practice tile) and \"Build targeted set\" (carousel action) all open the same stage. Three names for one thing teaches the student that names do not matter.\n- Fix: one door, one name. Keep the hero console. Turn the practice tile into something the console is not (recent sets, or saved sets).\n- Command: `$impeccable distill`\n\n**[P1] The hero does not get out of the way.**\n- Why: opening the launcher keeps the full 400px hero above it, so the student scrolls past \"Set up an LR drill\" (button) to reach \"Set up an LR drill\" (title). Same words, twice, 500px apart.\n- Fix: collapse the hero to a one-line band while a stage is open, with the greeting and a Cancel.\n- Command: `$impeccable layout`\n\n**[P1] The hub's identity is undocumented.**\n- Why: DESIGN.md sanctions rooms behind a wrapper class with their own brief. This room has the wrapper (`.drill-observatory-root`) and no brief, so every reviewer, and every future agent, will judge it against the Workroom's rules and fail it.\n- Fix: write `.impeccable/surfaces/drill-hub.md` stating the ground (cream stage, black cards), the families (Fraunces display, JetBrains labels, Outfit body), what glass is allowed to do, and the contrast floor. Then fix the code to match the brief, not the other way round.\n- Command: `$impeccable document`\n\n**[P2] \"Smart Drill\" is the one label that does not say what it does.**\n- Why: everything else on the page is literal. \"Smart\" asks the student to trust an adjective. The paragraph above it explains it; the button does not.\n- Fix: \"Start adaptive set\" or \"Drill my weakest types\".\n- Command: `$impeccable clarify`\n\n### Cognitive load\n\nChecklist failures: 4 of 8 (visible options at the first decision point 9, three routes to one action, primary action not distinguishable by weight alone on phone, progressive disclosure inverted since the launcher opens below a still-open hero). Critical band.\n\n### Emotional journey\n\nThe photo and the serif greeting land a calm, slightly ceremonial arrival. That is intentional and it works. The valley is the next 200px: nine controls and no verdict on what to do. Peak-end: the page ends on Today, which is the best moment on it, and the one most students will not reach.\n\n### Persona red flags\n\n**Celia (paying client, assigned work waiting).** Logs in to start what Joshua sent. Sees two CTAs about drills she was not assigned. Scrolls, finds \"Next up\" in the bottom-right card. On her phone, the assignment title is clipped mid-word. She was messaging Joshua about a pending drill status yesterday; nothing here tells her that either.\n\n**Alex (self-study power user).** Cmd-K works, typed addresses work, the switch has arrow keys. But Tab from the switch lands on Start Smart Drill with no visible ring, and the jump row's labels are invisible, so the keyboard-only path to a specific question is a guess.\n\n**Jordan (first session, phone).** Menu button covers the first thing on the page. \"Smart Drill\" and \"Build the drill you need\" both look like the assignment. Five-line console paragraph before the button. Today is four swipes down.\n\n### Minor observations\n\n- \"TUESDAY, SEPTEMBER 8\" here vs \"TUESDAY · SEPTEMBER 8 · 2026\" one click away on Home.\n- \"Insight preview\" for a new account is five near-empty slides on a seven-second timer. Consider hiding the carousel until there is a second real insight.\n- The launcher's loading state is a spinner; the filter frame could draw immediately.\n- Analytics and Journal are pills inside \"Practice tools\" though neither is a tool for practising; they are records.\n\n### Questions to consider\n\n- What if the hero were Today, and Smart Drill only appeared when nothing is assigned?\n- Does a student need to see LR/RC, jump, search and build before seeing what Joshua assigned?\n- If this room is allowed to be dark, glass and serif, where is that written, and what does the room owe the rest of the academy in return?\n- What would the page look like if it had one button?\n\n---\n\n## Recommended actions\n\n1. **[P0] `$impeccable harden`**: fix the jump row tone (`tone=\"light\"`), the CTA focus ring, the em-dash placeholder, and the 44px floor on switch options, selects, pills, dots and pause.\n2. **[P0] `$impeccable shape`**: decide whether Today's next step leads the hero. This is the product-principle call and needs Joshua's brief before layout work.\n3. **[P1] `$impeccable adapt`**: clear the fixed menu trigger under 1024px, fix the Today rail overflow at 375px, tighten the stacked hero on phones.\n4. **[P1] `$impeccable document`**: write the drill hub surface brief so the dark room is sanctioned, with its contrast floor and its allowed glass.\n5. **[P1] `$impeccable colorize`**: rebuild the ink ladder against measured contrast; nothing that carries text below 4.5:1.\n6. **[P1] `$impeccable distill`**: one door to the builder; collapse the hero while a stage is open.\n7. **[P2] `$impeccable clarify`**: \"Smart Drill\" label, \"Pick a test\" placeholders, year in the date.\n8. **[P2] `$impeccable extract`**: move the literal rgba and hex values in JSX and SectionToggle into the observatory tokens.\n9. **[P2] `$impeccable animate`**: replace the flip's height transition.\n10. **`$impeccable polish`**: final pass.\n\nRe-run `$impeccable audit` and `$impeccable critique` after fixes.\n"}

## assistant

Audit and critique are done. Full report saved at [wiki/DRILL-HUB-IMPECCABLE-AUDIT-2026-09-08.md](wiki/DRILL-HUB-IMPECCABLE-AUDIT-2026-09-08.md). Verified against production in the in-app browser, signed in as you, at desktop and 375px phone widths, with contrast, target sizes and focus measured in the live DOM.

**Scores**

| Report | Score | Band |
|---|---|---|
| Technical audit | 10/20 | Acceptable, significant work needed |
| Design critique (Nielsen) | 25/40 | Needs work |

**Verified defects, worst first**

- **The Test / Section / Question row is invisible.** It gets `tone="dark"` on the cream stage, so its labels measure 1.07:1. The switch beside it was fixed for exactly this and the jump row was not. One-line fix at [AcademyMainhub.tsx:400](projects/lsatu/src/pages/AcademyMainhub.tsx:400).
- **The fixed menu button covers content under 1024px.** It sits over the launcher title and the "Practice tools" label, and is 38px, under the 44px floor.
- **Start Smart Drill has no focus ring.** Measured outline none while focused. The observatory's `!important` overrides block the shared ring.
- **Phone overflow in Today.** Rails render 432px wide in a 375px viewport, so assignment titles are clipped mid-word.
- **Most micro-labels fail AA.** The ink ladder at 0.38 to 0.48 alpha lands between 3.2:1 and 4.2:1 on the card black. Inactive RC is 2.38:1.
- **An em-dash in student copy** in the builder's "Add exact questions" placeholder, against the PRODUCT.md commitment.

**Design verdict**

Handsome and honest, not decisive. The page shows nine controls before the thing you actually assigned, and on a phone Today's "Next up" is the fourth screen. That contradicts your own principle of computing the next step rather than presenting a grid. Three separate buttons open the same builder, and the hero stays fully open above the launcher, so "Set up an LR drill" appears twice 500px apart. The dark, glass, serif room also has no surface brief, so by DESIGN.md's rules it is a seam leak until one is written.

**What's working:** the LR/RC switch, copy that says what each button does, URL-carried state, honest empty states, a real pause on the carousel, lean WebP images.

**Skipped:** the `[Human]` browser overlay from the detector, because the browser pane was hidden and I could not inject the live server into it. I read the DOM directly instead, so nothing was left unmeasured.

Two decisions shape the fix order and only you can make them:

1. Should Today's assigned item lead the hero, with Smart Drill and Build as the fallback when nothing is assigned?
2. Is the drill hub a sanctioned dark room that gets its own surface brief, or should it flatten toward the Workroom?

The report ends with a ten-step command list. Say which of the two answers above you want and I'll start with `$impeccable harden` on the P0 and P1 defects, which need neither.

---
  const statusDelta = statusOrder(a.status) - statusOrder(b.status);
  if (statusDelta !== 0) return statusDelta;
  return new Date(b.updatedAt ?? 0).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt ?? 0).getTime();
}

function isActiveStatus(status: string) {
  return status === "assigned" || status === "in_progress" || status === "viewed" || status === "read" || status === "started";
}

function classroomItemToFocus(item: ClassroomItem): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!isActiveStatus(item.status)) return null;
  const route = resolveItemRoute(item);
  const due = dueLabel(item.due_at);
  const type: TodayFocusType = item.type === "bootcamp_module" ? "bootcamp" : "assignment";
  const isPlan = item.type === "weekly_plan_task";
  return {
    id: `classroom-${item.id}`,
    type,
    title: item.title,
    sourceLine:
      item.type === "bootcamp_module"
        ? "Bootcamp progress"
        : isPlan
          ? "Assigned in your study plan"
          : "Assigned by Joshua",
    status: due ?? STATUS_LABEL[item.status] ?? "Ready",
    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[item.status] ?? "Open",
    actionCue: due ?? undefined,
    route,
    priority: 900 + item.priority,
    updatedAt: item.due_at ?? item.created_at,
    preview: compactText(item.description, 110) ?? undefined,
    Icon: item.type === "bootcamp_module" || isPlan ? BookOpenCheck : ClipboardList,
  };
}

function homeworkToFocus(a: StudentAssignment): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;
  const questionCount = a.question_qids?.length ?? 0;
  return {
    id: `homework-${a.id}`,
    type: "assignment",
    title: a.set_title || "Homework",
    sourceLine: "Assigned by Joshua",
    status: STATUS_LABEL[a.status],
    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status],
    actionCue: questionCount > 0 ? `${questionCount} questions` : undefined,
    route: `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,
    priority: 820,
    progress: a.score != null ? `${Math.round(a.score)}%` : undefined,
    updatedAt: a.created_at,
    preview: compactText(a.set_description, 110) ?? undefined,
    Icon: ClipboardList,
  };
}

function taToFocus(a: StudentTAAssignment): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;
  return {
    id: `ta-${a.id}`,
    type: "assignment",
    title: a.title || "Instructor assignment",
    sourceLine: "Assigned by Joshua",
    status: STATUS_LABEL[a.status],
    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status],
    actionCue: a.pdf_url ? "PDF" : undefined,
    route: `/classroom/ta/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,
    priority: 840,
    updatedAt: a.assigned_at || a.created_at,
    Icon: BookOpenCheck,
  };
}

function guidedToFocus(
  a: GuidedAssignmentWithDrill,
  drillReturnTo: string,
): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;
  const count = a.guided_drill?.question_count;
  const minutes = a.guided_drill?.estimated_minutes;
  return {
    id: `guided-${a.id}`,
    type: "assignment",
    title: a.guided_drill?.title || "Guided drill",
    sourceLine: "Assigned guided practice",
    status: a.status === "assigned" ? "Ready" : "In progress",
    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status] ?? "Open",
    actionCue: minutes ? `${minutes} min` : count != null ? `${count} questions` : undefined,
    estimatedTime: minutes ? `${minutes} min` : undefined,
    route: `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(drillReturnTo)}`,
    priority: 830,
    updatedAt: a.assigned_at,
    Icon: ClipboardList,
  };
}

function instructorMessageToFocus(c: Conversation): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!c.unread || !c.last_message) return null;
  const fromInstructor = c.participants.some((p) => p.is_admin && p.user_id === c.last_message?.sender_id);
  if (!fromInstructor) return null;
  return {
    id: `conversation-${c.id}`,
    type: "message",
    title: c.subject || "Message from Joshua",
    sourceLine: "Message from Joshua",
    status: "New",
    actionLabel: "Reply",
    actionCue: "New",
    route: `/inbox/${encodeURIComponent(c.id)}`,
    priority: 700,
    preview: compactText(c.last_message.body, 92) ?? undefined,
    updatedAt: c.last_message.created_at,
    Icon: MessageSquare,
  };
}

function notificationToFocus(n: Notification, markRead: (id: string) => Promise<void>): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (n.read || n.type === "message") return null;
  return {
    id: `notification-${n.id}`,
    type: "notification",
    title: n.title,
    sourceLine: n.type === "assignment" ? "Assigned by Joshua" : "Notification",
    status: "New",
    actionLabel: n.link ? "Open" : "Review",
    actionCue: n.type === "assignment" ? "New" : undefined,
    route: n.link,
    priority: n.type === "assignment" ? 760 : 220,
    preview: compactText(n.body, 100) ?? undefined,
    updatedAt: n.created_at,
    Icon: n.type === "assignment" ? ClipboardList : Bell,
    onOpen: () => markRead(n.id),
  };
}

function lastActivityToFocus(
  row: LastActivityRow | null | undefined,
  drillReturnTo: string,
): TodayFocusItem | null {
  if (!row) return null;
  const snapshot = row.state_snapshot;
  const target = resolveResumeTarget(snapshot);
  const label = labelFor(snapshot);
  if (snapshot.kind === "bootcamp_step") {
    return {
      id: `last-${row.activity_id}`,
      type: "bootcamp",
      title: label,
      sourceLine: "Bootcamp progress",
      status: "In progress",
      actionLabel: "Continue",
      route: target.path,
      routeState: target.state,
      priority: 520,
      updatedAt: row.updated_at,
      Icon: BookOpenCheck,
    };
  }

  const total = snapshot.qids.length;
  const left = Math.max(0, total - snapshot.current_index);
  const progress = total > 0 ? `${Math.min(100, Math.round((snapshot.current_index / total) * 100))}%` : undefined;
  return {
    id: `last-${row.activity_id}`,
    type: snapshot.kind === "section" ? "set" : "drill",
    title: label,
    sourceLine: snapshot.kind === "section" ? "Active practice set" : "Unfinished drill",
    status: "In progress",
    actionLabel: "Resume",
    actionCue: left > 0 ? `${left} left` : undefined,
    route: target.path,
    routeState:
      target.path === "/drill"
        ? { ...(target.state as object), returnTo: drillReturnTo }
        : target.state,
    priority: snapshot.kind === "section" ? 480 : 460,
    progress,
    updatedAt: row.updated_at,
    Icon: snapshot.kind === "section" ? Signal : RotateCcw,
  };
}

function visibleRails(items: TodayFocusItem[], primaryId: string | null) {
  return items.filter((item) => item.id !== primaryId).slice(0, 2);
}

export default function TodayFocusPanel({
  drillReturnTo = "/classroom",
}: {
  drillReturnTo?: string;
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = React.useState(false);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);

  const {
    data: classroomItems = [],
    isLoading: classroomLoading,
    isError: classroomError,
  } = useClassroomItems();
  const { assignments: homework, loading: homeworkLoading, error: homeworkError } = useStudentAssignments();
  const { assignments: taAssignments, loading: taLoading, error: taError } = useStudentTAAssignments();
  const { notifications, loading: notificationsLoading, error: notificationsError, markRead } = useNotifications();
  const { conversations, loading: inboxLoading } = useInbox();
  const {
    data: lastActivity = null,
    isLoading: lastActivityLoading,
    isError: lastActivityError,
  } = useLastActivity();
  const {
    data: guided = [],
    isLoading: guidedLoading,
    isError: guidedError,
  } = useQuery<GuidedAssignmentWithDrill[]>({
    queryKey: ["guided-drill-assignments", user?.id],
    enabled: !!user,
    staleTime: 60_000,
    queryFn: async () => (
      await listAssignmentsForStudent(user!.id) as GuidedAssignmentWithDrill[]
    ),
  });

  const focusItems = React.useMemo(() => {
    const seenSourceIds = new Set<string>();
    const fromClassroom = classroomItems
      .map(classroomItemToFocus)
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);

    for (const item of classroomItems) {
      if (item.source_id) seenSourceIds.add(item.source_id);
    }

    const fromHomework = homework
      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))
      .map(homeworkToFocus)
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);
    const fromTa = taAssignments
      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))
      .map(taToFocus)
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);
    const fromGuided = guided
      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))
      .map((assignment) => guidedToFocus(assignment, drillReturnTo))
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);
    const fromNotifications = notifications
      .map((n) => notificationToFocus(n, markRead))
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);
    const fromMessages = conversations
      .map(instructorMessageToFocus)
      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);
    const fromLastActivity = lastActivityToFocus(lastActivity, drillReturnTo);

    const byId = new Map<string, TodayFocusItem>();
    for (const item of [
      ...fromClassroom,
      ...fromTa,
      ...fromGuided,
      ...fromHomework,
      ...fromNotifications,
      ...fromMessages,
      ...(fromLastActivity ? [fromLastActivity] : []),
    ]) {
      byId.set(item.id, item);
    }
    return Array.from(byId.values()).sort(itemSort);
  }, [classroomItems, conversations, drillReturnTo, guided, homework, lastActivity, markRead, notifications, taAssignments]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedId && !focusItems.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {
      setSelectedId(null);
    }
  }, [focusItems, selectedId]);

  const loading =
    classroomLoading ||
    homeworkLoading ||
    taLoading ||
    guidedLoading ||
    notificationsLoading ||
    inboxLoading ||
    lastActivityLoading;
  const hasError =
    classroomError ||
    !!homeworkError ||
    !!taError ||
    !!notificationsError ||
    guidedError ||
    lastActivityError;
  const primary = focusItems.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) ?? focusItems[0] ?? null;
  const rails = primary ? visibleRails(focusItems, primary.id) : [];

  const openItem = React.useCallback(
    async (item: TodayFocusItem) => {
      await item.onOpen?.();
      if (item.route) {
        navigate(item.route, item.routeState ? { state: item.routeState } : undefined);
      }
    },
    [navigate],
  );

              className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-ink-50"

              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">

function TodayPrimarySurface({
  item,
  expanded,
  onToggle,
  onActivate,
}: {
  item: TodayFocusItem;
  expanded: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
  onActivate: () => void;
}) {
  const Icon = item.Icon;
  // The card's surface — radius, glass, ring — is owned by
  // .drill-observatory-today-primary (and its `> div` rule) in classroom.css.
  return (
    <motion.div
      layout
      className="drill-observatory-today-primary group relative"
      transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 170, damping: 24 }}
    >
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={expanded}
          onClick={onToggle}
          className="relative flex w-full flex-col items-start px-5 pb-4 pt-5 text-left outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[rgba(244,241,234,0.55)] focus-visible:ring-inset"
        >
          <div className="flex w-full items-start justify-between gap-4">
            <span className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden />
              Next up
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {item.progress && (
                <span className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55">
                  {item.progress}
                </span>
              )}
              {item.status && (
                <span className="drill-observatory-today-chip">{item.status}</span>
              )}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-[26px] w-full">
            <h2 className="drill-observatory-today-title">{item.title}</h2>
            <p className="drill-observatory-ink-55 mt-[5px] text-[13px]">{item.sourceLine}</p>
            <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
              {expanded && (
                <motion.div
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -4 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -4 }}
                  className="drill-observatory-ink-62 mt-4 space-y-2 text-[13px] leading-6"
                >
                  <p>{contextLineFor(item)}</p>
                  {item.preview && (
                    <p className="drill-observatory-ink-85 line-clamp-2">
                      {item.type === "message" ? `"${item.preview}"` : item.preview}
                    </p>
                  )}
                  {(item.estimatedTime || item.progress) && (
                    <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-45">
                      {[item.estimatedTime, item.progress].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </AnimatePresence>
          </div>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={(event) => {
            event.stopPropagation();
            onActivate();
          }}
          disabled={!item.route && !item.onOpen}
          className="drill-observatory-today-action relative flex w-full items-center justify-between px-5 py-[13px] text-left text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-[#f4f1ea] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[rgba(244,241,234,0.55)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:text-[rgba(244,241,234,0.38)]"
        >
          <span>{actionText(item)}</span>
          <span className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55 flex items-center gap-2">
            {item.actionCue}
            <ArrowRight
              className="drill-observatory-ink-85 h-[15px] w-[15px] transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-0.5"
              strokeWidth={1.8}
              aria-hidden
            />
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function TodayRailItem({
  item,
  muted,
  onPromote,
}: {
  item: TodayFocusItem;
  muted: boolean;
  onPromote: () => void;
}) {
  const Icon = item.Icon;
  return (
    <motion.button
      type="button"
      layout
      onClick={onPromote}
      className={cn(
        "drill-observatory-today-rail",
        "flex w-full items-center gap-3 text-left outline-none",
        "focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-[rgba(244,241,234,0.55)]",
        muted && "opacity-60",
      )}
      transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 180, damping: 24 }}
    >
      <Icon
        className="drill-observatory-ink-55 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0"
        strokeWidth={1.8}
        aria-hidden
      />
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <span className="drill-observatory-ink-full block truncate text-[13px] font-medium">
          {railTitle(item)}
        </span>
        <span className="drill-observatory-ink-55 mt-0.5 block truncate text-[11.5px]">
          {item.sourceLine}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55 shrink-0">
        {item.status ?? item.actionLabel}
      </span>
    </motion.button>
  );
}

function TodaySkeleton() {
  return (
    <div aria-label="Loading today" className="animate-pulse">
      <div className="drill-observatory-today-primary min-h-[186px] p-5">
        <div className="h-2.5 w-16 rounded-full bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.14)]" />
        <div className="mt-8 h-6 w-3/4 rounded-full bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.12)]" />
        <div className="mt-3 h-3.5 w-1/2 rounded-full bg-[rgba(244,241,234,0.08)]" />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        <div className="drill-observatory-today-rail h-[52px]" />
        <div className="drill-observatory-today-rail h-[52px]" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function TodayEmptyState() {
  return (
    <div aria-live="polite">
      <div className="drill-observatory-today-primary px-5 pb-6 pt-5">
        <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55">
          Next up
        </p>
        <h2 className="drill-observatory-today-title mt-[26px]">All caught up</h2>
        <p className="drill-observatory-ink-55 mt-[5px] text-[13px]">
          Nothing assigned for today.
        </p>
        <p className="drill-observatory-ink-45 mt-6 text-[12.5px] leading-[1.45]">
          Assigned work and instructor messages will appear here when they exist.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        <EmptyRailQuiet />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function TodayErrorState() {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="drill-observatory-today-primary px-5 pb-6 pt-5">
        <p className="drill-observatory-label drill-observatory-label-sm drill-observatory-ink-55">
          Next up
        </p>
        <h2 className="drill-observatory-today-title mt-[26px]">
          Couldn&apos;t load today.
        </h2>
        <p className="drill-observatory-ink-55 mt-[5px] text-[13px]">
          Refresh the page to try again.
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        <div className="drill-observatory-today-quiet">
          Today is temporarily unavailable.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyRailQuiet() {
  return (
    <div className="drill-observatory-today-quiet">No secondary focus items.</div>
  );
}

function contextLineFor(item: TodayFocusItem) {
  if (item.type === "message") return "This is next because Joshua sent an unread message.";
  if (item.type === "bootcamp") return "This is next because you have bootcamp progress in motion.";
  if (item.type === "drill" || item.type === "set") return "This is next because you have unfinished practice.";
  if (item.type === "notification") return "This is next because a new notification is waiting.";
  return "This is next because it is assigned work.";
}

function actionText(item: TodayFocusItem) {
  if (item.actionLabel === "Start" && item.type === "bootcamp") return "Start lesson";
  if (item.actionLabel === "Practice") return "Practice";
  if (item.actionLabel === "Resume") return "Resume drill";
  return item.actionLabel;
}

function railTitle(item: TodayFocusItem) {
  if (item.type === "message") return "Message from Joshua";
  if (item.type === "drill") return "Smart LR Drill";
  if (item.type === "bootcamp") return "Bootcamp Lesson";
  return item.title;
}

import type { StudentAnalytics } from "@/lib/mainhubAnalytics";
import type { StudentStreak } from "@/hooks/useStudentAnalytics";

/** Which stable practice action a slide nudges the user toward. */
export type RecommendedAction = "full-section" | "type-drill" | null;

export interface InsightSlide {
  id: "streak" | "top-priority" | "accuracy-trend" | "practice-volume" | "wrong-answer";
  kicker: string;
  /** Primary line — the number/headline. Null when there is no data yet. */
  headline: string;
  detail: string;
  /** Label for the slide's own contextual action button. */
  actionLabel: string;
  /** Where the action button navigates (route or mainhub stage link). */
  actionHref: string;
  /** Analytics tab this slide deep-links into. */
  tab: string;
  /** Stable practice button to visually highlight while this slide shows. */
  recommend: RecommendedAction;
  /** True when the slide is showing a truthful empty state. */
  empty: boolean;
}

function fmtPct(v: number | null): string {
  return v === null ? "—" : `${Math.round(v)}%`;
}

const ANALYTICS = "/classroom/drill?stage=analytics";
const TYPE_DRILL = "/classroom/drill?stage=type-drill";
// Sitting a section lives in the Testing Centre, not the drill hub.
const FULL_SECTION = "/testing";

/**
 * Derives the rotating insight slides from real analytics + streak. Slides
 * always render (stable order) but each falls back to a truthful empty state
 * when its underlying data is missing, rather than inventing numbers.
 */
export function buildInsightSlides(
  analytics: StudentAnalytics | null,
  streak: StudentStreak | null,
): InsightSlide[] {
  const topPriority = analytics?.priorities[0] ?? null;
  const trend = analytics?.trendDelta ?? null;
  const volume7d =
    analytics?.volumeByDay.slice(-7).reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.attempts, 0) ?? 0;

  return [
    {
      id: "streak",
      kicker: "Streak",
      headline: streak && streak.current > 0 ? `${streak.current}-day streak` : "No streak yet",
      detail:
        streak && streak.current > 0
          ? streak.goalReached
            ? `Goal met today · best ${streak.longest} days`
            : `${Math.max(0, streak.goal - streak.questionsToday)} more today to keep it`
          : "Answer a few questions today to start one",
      actionLabel: "Practice today",
      actionHref: "/drill",
      tab: "overview",
      recommend: "full-section",
      empty: !streak || streak.current === 0,
    },
    {
      id: "top-priority",
      kicker: "Top priority",
      headline: topPriority ? topPriority.label : "No priority yet",
      detail: topPriority
        ? `${fmtPct(topPriority.firstAccuracy)} first-attempt · ${topPriority.firstAttempts} attempts`
        : "Practice more to surface your weakest type",
      actionLabel: "Build targeted set",
      actionHref: TYPE_DRILL,
      tab: "priorities",
      recommend: "type-drill",
      empty: !topPriority,
    },
    {
      id: "accuracy-trend",
      kicker: "Accuracy trend",
----

export default function QuestionFinder({ returnTo, tone = "light", bank }: QuestionFinderProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);

  /* Cmd/Ctrl+K is an accelerator on top of a visible, labelled control — never
     the only way in. It is ignored while the student is typing somewhere else,
     so it cannot steal a keystroke from a real field. */
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key.toLowerCase() !== "k" || !(event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey)) return;
      const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
      const tag = target?.tagName;
      if (!open && (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || target?.isContentEditable)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      setOpen((value) => !value);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);

  return (
    <div className="qf" data-tone={tone}>
      {/* ONE trigger, not one per breakpoint. Two buttons behind a CSS
          display swap would both stay in the accessibility tree, and a screen
          reader would announce "Find a question" twice on every visit. The
          label and the shortcut hint are what collapse on a phone; the control
          and its accessible name do not change. */}
      <button
        type="button"
        className="qf-trigger"
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        aria-haspopup="dialog"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-label="Find a question"
      >
        <Search className="qf-trigger-icon" size={15} strokeWidth={1.9} aria-hidden />
        <span className="qf-trigger-label" aria-hidden>
          Find a question
        </span>
        <span className="qf-trigger-kbd" aria-hidden>
          ⌘K
        </span>
      </button>

      {open && <FinderPanel returnTo={returnTo} tone={tone} bank={bank} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

.classroom-root .classroom-list-error p {
  font-size: 13px;
  margin: 0 0 10px;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-stage {
  background: var(--classroom-page);
  display: flex;
  flex: 1 1 auto;
  justify-content: center;
  min-width: 0;
  min-height: 100dvh;
  padding: 0 24px;
}

.classroom-root .classroom-column {
  max-width: 720px;
  padding: 80px 0 120px;
  width: 100%;
}

/* Schedule is an operational workspace, not a reading surface. Its calendar
   needs enough room for seven honest columns plus a selected-day agenda. */
.classroom-root.classroom-root-schedule .classroom-column {
  max-width: 1240px;
  padding: 48px 0 96px;
}

/* Reserves the stage while a code-split route resolves, so the rail never sits
   beside a collapsed column. See StageFallback in ClassroomLayout.tsx. */
.classroom-root .classroom-stage-pending {
  min-height: 100dvh;
  width: 100%;
}

/* Route changes move the stage's geometry: Home is a 1260px dashboard with no
   stage padding, Drill is full-bleed at 8px, everything else is a 720px reading
   column. Untransitioned, that swap lands as a jump the moment the new route
   paints. --classroom-transition is the same 300ms the rail and panel wash
   already use, so this reads as one motion rather than a new one. */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
  .classroom-root .classroom-stage {
    transition:
      background var(--classroom-transition) ease,
      padding var(--classroom-transition) ease;
  }

  .classroom-root .classroom-column {
    transition:
      max-width var(--classroom-transition) ease,
      padding var(--classroom-transition) ease;
  }

  /* Content arrives on top of that geometry settling. A short opacity/transform
     lift (compositor-only, no layout) turns the pop into a crossfade. 160ms
     matches the observatory's own --observatory-motion. */
  .classroom-root .classroom-stage > .classroom-column > *,
  .classroom-root .classroom-messages-stage > *,
  .classroom-root .classroom-workspace-stage > * {
----
    padding: 24px 18px 48px;
  }
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-stage {
  background: var(--classroom-drill-page);
  padding: 8px 8px 8px 0;
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-column {
  max-width: none;
  padding: 8px;
}

/* Drill landing workspace: The Reasoning Observatory.
   Scoped here so the official /drill question surface cannot inherit it.

   2026-08-01 reskin ("hyper-real imagery"): the page is now ivory-on-black with
   two photographic surfaces (a planet horizon behind the hero, an eclipse behind
   Today) doing all the accent work. There is no accent colour on this page — no
   blue, no amber, no gold. Every grid texture, radial wash and gradient tone map
   the old observatory carried has been removed; what is left is flat tinted
   black, hairline rings, glass, and the two images. */
/* .rc-page is the Reading Comprehension hub, which is its own route rather than
   a classroom stage but belongs to the same visual system. It hosts the ladder
   here rather than restating the values, so there is still one source. */
.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill,
.rc-page {
  /* Ink. One ivory, stepped by opacity. The ladder is closed — if a value is not
     on it, it does not belong on this page. */
  --observatory-ink: #f4f1ea;
  --observatory-ink-90: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.9);
  --observatory-ink-85: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.85);
  --observatory-ink-70: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.7);
  --observatory-ink-62: rgba(244, 241, 234, 0.62);
----
  --observatory-label-font: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, monospace;
  --observatory-display-font: "Fraunces", ui-serif, Georgia, serif;

  /* Motion: 150-180ms, standard easing, transform and opacity only. */
  --observatory-motion: 160ms cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-stage {
  padding: 24px 24px 32px 16px;
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-column {
  padding: 0;
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-sidebar-card {
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 100% 0%, rgba(255, 138, 61, 0.045), transparent 32%),
    var(--classroom-panel);
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-nav-active {
  position: relative;
}

.classroom-root.classroom-root-drill .classroom-nav-active::after {

  border-radius: 999px;

/* The Drill landing workspace is a near-black surface. Its trigger takes the
   dark tone directly; the panel gets it through the portal so the overlay does
   not flash a warm-paper card over a black page. */
.qf[data-tone="dark"],
html.dark .qf {
  --qf-ink: #f5f1ec;
  --qf-ink-mid: #cfc8c0;
  --qf-body: #b3aca4;
  --qf-soft: #9b948d;
  --qf-muted: #8b837b;

  --qf-surface: #17161c;
  --qf-surface-warm: #1a1820;
  --qf-row-hover: #221f28;
  --qf-sunk: #121116;

  --qf-hair-06: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  --qf-hair-08: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  --qf-hair-12: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.14);
}

      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 @[980px]:grid @[980px]:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_320px] @[980px]:gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col min-h-0">
      {/* Filter Bar */}
      <Card className="mb-5 overflow-visible border-border/70 bg-card/80 shadow-[0_16px_40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.18)]">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-4">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-1" aria-live="polite">
              <p className="text-lg font-semibold tracking-[-0.015em]">Filter the question bank</p>
              <p className="text-sm tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">
                {totalCount.toLocaleString()} matching {totalCount === 1 ? 'question' : 'questions'}
              </p>
            </div>
            {hasAnyFilter && (
              <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="h-9 px-3" onClick={clearAllFilters}>
                Clear all
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
          {/* Filter Controls
              Item D order: PrepTest → Section → Q# → Type → Reasoning →
              Features → Levels → Search. Reordered from the legacy layout
              (PT → Type → Reasoning → Features → Section → Levels → Search). */}
          <div className="space-y-5 border-t border-border/60 px-5 py-5">
            {/* Spec line. Replaces a single `Q#` number input that could hold
                exactly one value — so "Q1, 6, 8, 23", the thing you actually
                want when rebuilding a set, was not expressible at all.

                It owns PT, section and question number only. Type, reasoning,
                features and levels stay in their popovers, because a bar that
                round-tripped through a grammar unable to express them would
                drop them silently on every commit. */}
            <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[104px_minmax(0,1fr)] sm:gap-4">
              {/* "Spec" was the internal name for the grammar and it read as
                  jargon on screen: it sat above SOURCE and FOCUS, which name
                  what you are choosing, while this one named the machinery.
                  The row is the typed alternative to the popovers below, so it
                  says that. Each label carries a hint for the same reason —
                  three bare nouns in a column give no order to follow. */}
              <div className="sm:pt-3">
                <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-foreground/75">
                  Type it
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground">
                  Faster than clicking
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 space-y-2">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <Input
                      value={specText}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        setSpecText(e.target.value);
                        if (specIssues.length > 0) setSpecIssues([]);
                      }}
                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                          e.preventDefault();
                          commitSpec();
                        }
                      }}
                      onBlur={commitSpec}
                      placeholder="PT 101-110 S2,3 Q1,6,8,23"
                      aria-label="Type a PrepTest, section and question range"

  it("renders the hero, practice + insight stage, and today's focus panel", async () => {
    const queryClient = new QueryClient({
      defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } },
    });

    render(
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <MemoryRouter initialEntries={["/classroom/drill"]} future={{ v7_relativeSplatPath: true, v7_startTransition: true }}>
          <AcademyMainhub />
        </MemoryRouter>
      </QueryClientProvider>,
    );

    expect(screen.queryByRole("heading", { name: "LR Smart Drill" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Start Smart Drill" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    // The Spline robot was cut in the 2026-08-01 reskin: the hero is two columns
    // (greeting + finder console) over the horizon image, with no third bay.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Robot scene")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Build the drill you need." })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Set up an LR drill" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("combobox", { name: "PrepTest" })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Advanced/i })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Practice and insight workspace" }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Practice tools")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Build a Set")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Sitting a section is the Testing Centre's job. Two doors to the same
    // thing is what made this hub feel packed.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Full Section")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Insight preview")).toBeInTheDocument();
    // The Question finder's second mount. Without it, a student standing on the
    // page that exists for picking questions has to go back to Home to search.
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Find a question" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Today" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "All caught up" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText("Nothing assigned for today.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Orbital workspace")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("region", { name: "Mainhub orbital workspace" }),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Set up an LR drill" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Unified LR drill builder" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("offers Build a Set named for the RC bank, and does not offer a section to sit", () => {
    renderHub("/classroom/drill?section=rc");
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: "Build a Set Choose passages by topic, format, and difficulty.",
      }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    // Sitting a real section lives in the Testing Centre, in RC as in LR.
    expect(screen.queryByText("Full Section")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /Search passages/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    // The bay is shared, but the panel under the tools is not: the carousel is
    // built from LR attempts, so RC shows its own drill history instead.
    expect(screen.getByText("Your reading drills")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Insight preview")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("keeps section choice in the LR/RC switch instead of repeating it as a practice tile", () => {
    renderHub("/classroom/drill");
    expect(
      screen.queryByRole("button", {
        name: "Reading Comprehension Drill full passages the way they appear on the test.",
      }),
    ).toBeNull();

    fireEvent.click(rcSwitch());
    expect(url().get("section")).toBe("rc");
    expect(screen.getByText("Practice tools")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

PRODUCT.md's second principle: compute the\n * next step, do not present a grid. The hero leads with the primary item; the\n * panel carries the queue behind it. Both must agree on which item is first,\n * and the only way to guarantee that is one computation.\n */\n\ntype GuidedAssignmentWithDrill = GuidedDrillAssignment & { guided_drill: GuidedDrill };\n\nexport type TodayFocusType =\n  | \"assignment\"\n  | \"message\"\n  | \"bootcamp\"\n  | \"set\"\n  | \"drill\"\n  | \"notification\";\n\nexport type TodayActionLabel = \"Start\" | \"Continue\" | \"Reply\" | \"Resume\" | \"Review\" | \"Practice\" | \"Open\";\n\nexport interface TodayFocusItem {\n  id: string;\n  type: TodayFocusType;\n  title: string;\n  sourceLine: string;\n  status?: string;\n  actionLabel: TodayActionLabel;\n  actionCue?: string;\n  route?: string | null;\n  routeState?: unknown;\n  priority: number;\n  preview?: string;\n  progress?: string;\n  estimatedTime?: string;\n  updatedAt?: string;\n  Icon: React.ElementType;\n  onOpen?: () => void | Promise<void>;\n}\n\nconst ACTION_BY_STATUS: Record<string, TodayActionLabel> = {\n  assigned: \"Start\",\n  in_progress: \"Continue\",\n  viewed: \"Continue\",\n  read: \"Continue\",\n  started: \"Continue\",\n  completed: \"Review\",\n  draft: \"Open\",\n  archived: \"Open\",\n};\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<AssignmentStatus | TAAssignmentStatus | ClassroomItem[\"status\"], string> = {\n  assigned: \"Ready\",\n  in_progress: \"In progress\",\n  viewed: \"In progress\",\n  completed: \"Done\",\n  draft: \"Draft\",\n  archived: \"Archived\",\n};\n\nfunction compactText(text: string | null | undefined, max = 92) {\n  const value = text?.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim();\n  if (!value) return null;\n  return value.length > max ? `${value.slice(0, max - 3)}...` : value;\n}\n\nfunction dueLabel(dueAt: string | null | undefined) {\n  if (!dueAt) return null;\n  const date = new Date(dueAt);\n  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return null;\n  return isToday(date) ? \"Due today\" : `Due ${format(date, \"MMM d\")}`;\n}\n\nfunction statusOrder(status?: string) {\n  if (status === \"In progress\") return 0;\n  if (status === \"Ready\") return 1;\n  if (status === \"Done\") return 3;\n  return 2;\n}\n\nfunction itemSort(a: TodayFocusItem, b: TodayFocusItem) {\n  const priorityDelta = b.priority - a.priority;\n  if (priorityDelta !== 0) return priorityDelta;\n  const statusDelta = statusOrder(a.status) - statusOrder(b.status);\n  if (statusDelta !== 0) return statusDelta;\n  return new Date(b.updatedAt ?? 0).getTime() - new Date(a.updatedAt ?? 0).getTime();\n}\n\nfunction isActiveStatus(status: string) {\n  return status === \"assigned\" || status === \"in_progress\" || status === \"viewed\" || status === \"read\" || status === \"started\";\n}\n\nfunction classroomItemToFocus(item: ClassroomItem): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!isActiveStatus(item.status)) return null;\n  const route = resolveItemRoute(item);\n  const due = dueLabel(item.due_at);\n  const type: TodayFocusType = item.type === \"bootcamp_module\" ? \"bootcamp\" : \"assignment\";\n  const isPlan = item.type === \"weekly_plan_task\";\n  return {\n    id: `classroom-${item.id}`,\n    type,\n    title: item.title,\n    sourceLine:\n      item.type === \"bootcamp_module\"\n        ? \"Bootcamp progress\"\n        : isPlan\n          ? \"Assigned in your study plan\"\n          : \"Assigned by Joshua\",\n    status: due ?? STATUS_LABEL[item.status] ?? \"Ready\",\n    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[item.status] ?? \"Open\",\n    actionCue: due ?? undefined,\n    route,\n    priority: 900 + item.priority,\n    updatedAt: item.due_at ?? item.created_at,\n    preview: compactText(item.description, 110) ?? undefined,\n    Icon: item.type === \"bootcamp_module\" || isPlan ? BookOpenCheck : ClipboardList,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction homeworkToFocus(a: StudentAssignment): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;\n  const questionCount = a.question_qids?.length ?? 0;\n  return {\n    id: `homework-${a.id}`,\n    type: \"assignment\",\n    title: a.set_title || \"Homework\",\n    sourceLine: \"Assigned by Joshua\",\n    status: STATUS_LABEL[a.status],\n    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status],\n    actionCue: questionCount > 0 ? `${questionCount} questions` : undefined,\n    route: `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\n    priority: 820,\n    progress: a.score != null ? `${Math.round(a.score)}%` : undefined,\n    updatedAt: a.created_at,\n    preview: compactText(a.set_description, 110) ?? undefined,\n    Icon: ClipboardList,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction taToFocus(a: StudentTAAssignment): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;\n  return {\n    id: `ta-${a.id}`,\n    type: \"assignment\",\n    title: a.title || \"Instructor assignment\",\n    sourceLine: \"Assigned by Joshua\",\n    status: STATUS_LABEL[a.status],\n    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status],\n    actionCue: a.pdf_url ? \"PDF\" : undefined,\n    route: `/classroom/ta/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}`,\n    priority: 840,\n    updatedAt: a.assigned_at || a.created_at,\n    Icon: BookOpenCheck,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction guidedToFocus(\n  a: GuidedAssignmentWithDrill,\n  drillReturnTo: string,\n): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!isActiveStatus(a.status)) return null;\n  const count = a.guided_drill?.question_count;\n  const minutes = a.guided_drill?.estimated_minutes;\n  return {\n    id: `guided-${a.id}`,\n    type: \"assignment\",\n    title: a.guided_drill?.title || \"Guided drill\",\n    sourceLine: \"Assigned guided practice\",\n    status: a.status === \"assigned\" ? \"Ready\" : \"In progress\",\n    actionLabel: ACTION_BY_STATUS[a.status] ?? \"Open\",\n    actionCue: minutes ? `${minutes} min` : count != null ? `${count} questions` : undefined,\n    estimatedTime: minutes ? `${minutes} min` : undefined,\n    route: `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(a.id)}?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent(drillReturnTo)}`,\n    priority: 830,\n    updatedAt: a.assigned_at,\n    Icon: ClipboardList,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction instructorMessageToFocus(c: Conversation): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!c.unread || !c.last_message) return null;\n  const fromInstructor = c.participants.some((p) => p.is_admin && p.user_id === c.last_message?.sender_id);\n  if (!fromInstructor) return null;\n  return {\n    id: `conversation-${c.id}`,\n    type: \"message\",\n    title: c.subject || \"Message from Joshua\",\n    sourceLine: \"Message from Joshua\",\n    status: \"New\",\n    actionLabel: \"Reply\",\n    actionCue: \"New\",\n    route: `/inbox/${encodeURIComponent(c.id)}`,\n    priority: 700,\n    preview: compactText(c.last_message.body, 92) ?? undefined,\n    updatedAt: c.last_message.created_at,\n    Icon: MessageSquare,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction notificationToFocus(n: Notification, markRead: (id: string) => Promise<void>): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (n.read || n.type === \"message\") return null;\n  return {\n    id: `notification-${n.id}`,\n    type: \"notification\",\n    title: n.title,\n    sourceLine: n.type === \"assignment\" ? \"Assigned by Joshua\" : \"Notification\",\n    status: \"New\",\n    actionLabel: n.link ? \"Open\" : \"Review\",\n    actionCue: n.type === \"assignment\" ? \"New\" : undefined,\n    route: n.link,\n    priority: n.type === \"assignment\" ? 760 : 220,\n    preview: compactText(n.body, 100) ?? undefined,\n    updatedAt: n.created_at,\n    Icon: n.type === \"assignment\" ? ClipboardList : Bell,\n    onOpen: () => markRead(n.id),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction lastActivityToFocus(\n  row: LastActivityRow | null | undefined,\n  drillReturnTo: string,\n): TodayFocusItem | null {\n  if (!row) return null;\n  const snapshot = row.state_snapshot;\n  const target = resolveResumeTarget(snapshot);\n  const label = labelFor(snapshot);\n  if (snapshot.kind === \"bootcamp_step\") {\n    return {\n      id: `last-${row.activity_id}`,\n      type: \"bootcamp\",\n      title: label,\n      sourceLine: \"Bootcamp progress\",\n      status: \"In progress\",\n      actionLabel: \"Continue\",\n      route: target.path,\n      routeState: target.state,\n      priority: 520,\n      updatedAt: row.updated_at,\n      Icon: BookOpenCheck,\n    };\n  }\n\n  const total = snapshot.qids.length;\n  const left = Math.max(0, total - snapshot.current_index);\n  const progress = total > 0 ? `${Math.min(100, Math.round((snapshot.current_index / total) * 100))}%` : undefined;\n  return {\n    id: `last-${row.activity_id}`,\n    type: snapshot.kind === \"section\" ? \"set\" : \"drill\",\n    title: label,\n    sourceLine: snapshot.kind === \"section\" ? \"Active practice set\" : \"Unfinished drill\",\n    status: \"In progress\",\n    actionLabel: \"Resume\",\n    actionCue: left > 0 ? `${left} left` : undefined,\n    route: target.path,\n    routeState:\n      target.path === \"/drill\"\n        ? { ...(target.state as object), returnTo: drillReturnTo }\n        : target.state,\n    priority: snapshot.kind === \"section\" ? 480 : 460,\n    progress,\n    updatedAt: row.updated_at,\n    Icon: snapshot.kind === \"section\" ? Signal : RotateCcw,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction visibleRails(items: TodayFocusItem[], primaryId: string | null) {\n  return items.filter((item) => item.id !== primaryId).slice(0, 2);\n}\n\nexport interface TodayFocus {\n  items: TodayFocusItem[];\n  primary: TodayFocusItem | null;\n  rails: TodayFocusItem[];\n  loading: boolean;\n  hasError: boolean;\n  /** Make a rail item the primary one, without navigating. */\n  promote: (id: string) => void;\n  /** Follow an item's route, running its side effect (e.g. mark read) first. */\n  open: (item: TodayFocusItem) => Promise<void>;\n}\n\nexport function useTodayFocus(drillReturnTo = \"/classroom\"): TodayFocus {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { user } = useAuth();\n  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = React.useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const {\n    data: classroomItems = [],\n    isLoading: classroomLoading,\n    isError: classroomError,\n  } = useClassroomItems();\n  const { assignments: homework, loading: homeworkLoading, error: homeworkError } = useStudentAssignments();\n  const { assignments: taAssignments, loading: taLoading, error: taError } = useStudentTAAssignments();\n  const { notifications, loading: notificationsLoading, error: notificationsError, markRead } = useNotifications();\n  const { conversations, loading: inboxLoading } = useInbox();\n  const {\n    data: lastActivity = null,\n    isLoading: lastActivityLoading,\n    isError: lastActivityError,\n  } = useLastActivity();\n  const {\n    data: guided = [],\n    isLoading: guidedLoading,\n    isError: guidedError,\n  } = useQuery<GuidedAssignmentWithDrill[]>({\n    queryKey: [\"guided-drill-assignments\", user?.id],\n    enabled: !!user,\n    staleTime: 60_000,\n    queryFn: async () => (\n      await listAssignmentsForStudent(user!.id) as GuidedAssignmentWithDrill[]\n    ),\n  });\n\n  const items = React.useMemo(() => {\n    const seenSourceIds = new Set<string>();\n    const fromClassroom = classroomItems\n      .map(classroomItemToFocus)\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n\n    for (const item of classroomItems) {\n      if (item.source_id) seenSourceIds.add(item.source_id);\n    }\n\n    const fromHomework = homework\n      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))\n      .map(homeworkToFocus)\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n    const fromTa = taAssignments\n      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))\n      .map(taToFocus)\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n    const fromGuided = guided\n      .filter((a) => !seenSourceIds.has(a.id))\n      .map((assignment) => guidedToFocus(assignment, drillReturnTo))\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n    const fromNotifications = notifications\n      .map((n) => notificationToFocus(n, markRead))\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n    const fromMessages = conversations\n      .map(instructorMessageToFocus)\n      .filter((item): item is TodayFocusItem => item !== null);\n    const fromLastActivity = lastActivityToFocus(lastActivity, drillReturnTo);\n\n    const byId = new Map<string, TodayFocusItem>();\n    for (const item of [\n      ...fromClassroom,\n      ...fromTa,\n      ...fromGuided,\n      ...fromHomework,\n      ...fromNotifications,\n      ...fromMessages,\n      ...(fromLastActivity ? [fromLastActivity] : []),\n    ]) {\n      byId.set(item.id, item);\n    }\n    return Array.from(byId.values()).sort(itemSort);\n  }, [classroomItems, conversations, drillReturnTo, guided, homework, lastActivity, markRead, notifications, taAssignments]);\n\n  React.useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedId && !items.some((item) => item.id === selectedId)) {\n      setSelectedId(null);\n    }\n  }, [items, selectedId]);\n\n  const loading =\n    classroomLoading ||\n    homeworkLoading ||\n    taLoading ||\n    guidedLoading ||\n    notificationsLoading ||\n    inboxLoading ||\n    lastActivityLoading;\n  const hasError =\n    classroomError ||\n    !!homeworkError ||\n    !!taError ||\n    !!notificationsError ||\n    guidedError ||\n    lastActivityError;\n  const primary = items.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) ?? items[0] ?? null;\n  const rails = React.useMemo(\n    () => (primary ? visibleRails(items, primary.id) : []),\n    [items, primary],\n  );\n\n  const open = React.useCallback(\n    async (item: TodayFocusItem) => {\n      await item.onOpen?.();\n      if (item.route) {\n        navigate(item.route, item.routeState ? { state: item.routeState } : undefined);\n      }\n    },\n    [navigate],\n  );\n\n  const promote = React.useCallback((id: string) => setSelectedId(id), []);\n\n  return { items, primary, rails, loading, hasError, promote, open };\n}\n\n/** Copy shared by the hero and the panel, so the two never describe one item two ways. */\nexport function actionText(item: TodayFocusItem) {\n  if (item.actionLabel === \"Start\" && item.type === \"bootcamp\") return \"Start lesson\";\n  if (item.actionLabel === \"Practice\") return \"Practice\";\n  if (item.actionLabel === \"Resume\") return \"Resume drill\";\n  return item.actionLabel;\n}\n\nexport function contextLineFor(item: TodayFocusItem) {\n  if (item.type === \"message\") return \"This is next because Joshua sent an unread message.\";\n  if (item.type === \"bootcamp\") return \"This is next because you have bootcamp progress in motion.\";\n  if (item.type === \"drill\" || item.type === \"set\") return \"This is next because you have unfinished practice.\";\n  if (item.type === \"notification\") return \"This is next because a new notification is waiting.\";\n  return \"This is next because it is assigned work.\";\n}\n\nexport function railTitle(item: TodayFocusItem) {\n  if (item.type === \"message\") return \"Message from Joshua\";\n  if (item.type === \"drill\") return \"Smart LR Drill\";\n  if (item.type === \"bootcamp\") return \"Bootcamp Lesson\";\n  return item.title;\n}\n"}

<TodayEmptyState />\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TodayPrimarySurface({\n  item,\n  expanded,\n  onToggle,\n  onActivate,\n}: {\n  item: (\n          <RcSessionHistory tone=\"dark\" />\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"drill-observatory-insights\" aria-label=\"Insights\">\n            {shown.map((slide) => (\n              <li \"…\" : slide.headline}\n                  </span>\n                  <span className=\"drill-observatory-ink-62 mt-1 block text-[12.5px] font-normal leading-[1.45]\">\n                    {slide.detail}\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => handleSlideAction(slide)}\n                  className=\"drill-observatory-insight-action inline-flex items-center gap-2\"\n                >\n                  {slide.actionLabel}\n                  <ArrowRight className=\"h-[13px] w-[13px]\" strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden />\n                </button>\n              </li>\n            ))}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {(canOpenAnalytics || canOpenJournal) && (\n        <div className=\"drill-observatory-records mt-auto flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-5\">\n          {canOpenAnalytics && (\n            <SecondaryLink icon={BarChart3} label=\"Analytics\"          )}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function PracticeTile({\n  icon: Icon,\n  title,\n  description,\n  onClick,\n}: {\n  icon: React.ElementType;\n  title: string;\n <div>\n        <div className=\"drill-observatory-ink-full text-[15px] font-semibold\">{title}</div>\n        <div className=\"drill-observatory-ink-62 mt-[3px] text-[12.5px] leading-[1.45]\">\n          {description}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function SecondaryLink({\n  icon: Icon,\n  label,\n  onClick,\n}: {\n  icon: React.ElementType;\n  label: string;\n
